  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Josef正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Otto Weiss挡穿堂风。Otto Weiss在旧布底下翻了个身，Josef立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Katharina Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Otto Weiss，再看见Josef冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1920.02** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Josef把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Mitzi挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Mitzi转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Josef站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1920.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Josef跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Josefine Hofer从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Otto Winter 2家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Otto Winter 2端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1921.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anton Bauer从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Josef答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Josef回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Anton Bauer看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Emil Weiss朝Josef招手。Josef挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Josef把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Mitzi Lechner在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Mitzi Lechner数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.04** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Emil终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Emil说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Emil看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.02_

- **1925.01** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1925.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Otto Weiss把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Josef把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Josef只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Otto Weiss在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child; condition_

  Friedrich Stein先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:54; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.06** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Berta Winter说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josef Bauer来签字。Josef Bauer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Josef把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1925.08** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Josef把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Josef坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; skill; mood -0.05000000000000002; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

  Josefine Hofer说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Josef Bauer从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Josef解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Helene Lechner也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Josef自己晾。Josef把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Helene Lechner说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Helene Lechner哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Josef站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Grete Stein追出来说座位可以保留两周，Josef点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1927.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-resented-drilling` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Otto Weiss把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Josef把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Otto Weiss自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Josef面前，不说话。Josef看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。
  _effects: flag +sibling_resented_tutoring; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child_

- **1927.04** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/excluded` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton说入伙要交二十个铜板买蜡烛，Josef掏遍口袋只有九个。他们让他在楼梯口等，等到天黑也没人下来。楼上传来笑声，像隔着一层厚被子。Josef把九个铜板在楼梯上排成一排，又一个个收进口袋。
  _effects: mood -0.12000000000000005; flag +excluded_from_friend_group; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002799_

- **1927.09** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Josef的手，又让Josef推两下刨子试试。Josef用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josef Bauer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.10000000000000002; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/went-once-and-left` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef交了第一个月的会费，进去坐了一刻钟。屋里太吵，下棋的争论盖过了念书的声音，他借的书也被人翻得卷了边。下个月他路过门口，没再进去交十赫勒。

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Anton Weiss把Josef叫到巷口，问Otto Weiss叫什么、住在哪间屋。Josef答到一半就停住了。Anton Weiss说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Josef回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Otto Weiss正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Josef。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000006; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1929.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  旧货贩子把外套翻过来看了看里布，伸出两个手指。Josef接过两枚克朗，塞进鞋垫底下，走回家时脚心一直硌着。晚上Josef Bauer站在门后，手搭在空衣钩上，问外套呢。Josef说不知道，声音比平时高了一点。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1929.11** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/denied` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  墙上出现了一行关于Josef的脏话，Grete Stein让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Anton在背后学那句话的腔调，Josef回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-by-the-day` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne只招日工。Josef天亮前到门口，名字写在临时名单上，干一天算一天，一天一克朗，月底结二十六克朗。工头说下个月可能有正式位置，也可能没有；Josef把每天的工时记在烟盒背面，怕漏掉一天。
  _effects: employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; role; cash 2 (first daily pay advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +day_labour_only; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  Josef和Gustav Huber 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Huber 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Alois Bauer 3 的话很简短：名单不能批。Josef 把表格折好，转头去搬砖搭灶。Grete Stein 从家里抱来一捆柴，火星溅在围裙上，拍两下就灭了。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1930.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Josef Bauer在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; flag +recovered_with_kin_care; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food_

- **1931.07** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Ferdinand Winter合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1931.08** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Emil敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Emil什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Gustav Huber 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Josef留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Josef在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Josef的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price（其中25克朗记入债务）); health 0.040000000000000036 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1932.08** `r2-faith.clothing-drive-sort::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reported-to-the-priest` tags: charity, clothing_drive, found_money, honesty, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef没有动那本存折，先拿去给Rosa Winter 2看。Rosa Winter 2翻到最后一页，说户主生前每月给孤儿院捐一克朗。Josef当天下午把存折送到城南，女儿接过时没有哭，只问母亲最后穿的是哪件外套。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +reported_found_bankbook_to_parish; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; knowledge “the dead woman gave one krone a month to the orphanage”_

- **1932.11** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-the-wallet` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, reform, medical
  Josef把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Josef把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。
  _effects: cash 3 (the money in the lost wallet); state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:124; flag +kept_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Otto Weiss差三分。回家路上，Otto Weiss一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Josef把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Josef听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Otto Weiss出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Josef能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Josef的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Kral的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Josef在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Josef一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 25 -> 31; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `major-task.scholarship-application/half-fee-place` tags: task, task:education, education, scholarship, school
  委员会只肯减一半学费。Grete Stein替Josef争到六克朗书费，条件是走读，每天放学后替学校抄名册。名册要抄到天黑，手指常被蓝墨水染蓝。Josef算过车票和墨水钱，还是不够，只好在印刷厂接晚间折页活，把课本钱先挣出来。
  _effects: education higher; cash 6 (book allowance from the school fund); role; flag +works_evenings_for_school; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

  Josef在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1934.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household

  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Josef把钱包塞进大衣内袋，用那九克朗买了两袋煤、一双不漏水的靴子和半磅咖啡。第二天，歌剧院门口贴出一张手写启事，说一位老妇人在找一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面是她丈夫留下的最后一张站票。Josef路过时把围巾往上拉了拉，靴子很暖，走路却没有声音。
  _effects: cash 9 (留下的钱包里的钱); state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 31 -> 46; condition; flag +kept_found_wallet; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 46 -> 58; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 11; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 58 -> 68; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙); health -0.01999999999999999 (在冻雨里走回家); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Josef在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Rosa Winter第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中17.6克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Josef把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Josef在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.04000000000000001 (营养不足和夜里缝补的劳累)_

- **1938.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.11** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.default` tags: home, market, food, social

  _effects: food; reputation -0.15; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1938.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Josef在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖（其中0.03克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Josef Winter攥着Josef的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08000000000000002 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.05999999999999994; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +novel_shelved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001113:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1942.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  _effects: cash 15 (舅父借给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; flag +owes_uncle_15_kronen; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; flag +rent_installment_plan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001629:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  七点刚过，Emil Huber照例来买一只 Semmel。Josef把紫罗兰和两只 Semmel 放在柜台上，先付了四十赫勒，又把上回算错的账一笔一笔说清：借出去的是八克朗，记成了十克朗，是自己记错了。Emil Huber听完没马上说话，把零钱数进围裙口袋，最后说中午过来喝碗汤。

  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Josef的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Alois Bauer在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱)_

  Josef看见Gustav Kral在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Josef的腰在门框上撞了一下，Gustav Kral从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.03 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Grete Winter从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Josef买双能进厂的结实靴子。Josef数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子)_

  柜脚磕在石阶边缘那一声，Anna Winter在楼上就听见了。下来摸着裂缝，说这柜子跟了十几年，你们抬东西也太毛躁。Josef把手在裤子上蹭了蹭，血印子蹭出一条。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (搬家磕坏柜脚被扣工钱); health -0.019999999999999997 (搬柜子时手背划伤)_

  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Otto Winter 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; flag +parish_registered_

- **1946.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Josef攥紧扶手。Otto Moser站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010993_

  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Friedrich Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1947.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Josef把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001012:child_

  Alois Bauer说补助金只发给有家属的人，Josef单身，只能去修路队领工钱。第二天Josef扛着铁锹站在城郊，和几十个男人一起把碎砖填进车辙。工钱按日结，比补助多两克朗，但一天下来，手掌磨出了三个水泡。
  _effects: cash 2 (补助金窗口的第二张表); health -0.03 (补助金窗口的第二张表); condition; flag +work13_sent_to_relief_work_

  Josef第二次去时，Gustav Stein找不到上次的登记卡。柜子里堆着三个月的表格，Gustav Stein翻了半小时，说可能送到了区里。Anna Kral 2记得Josef的名字，却记不得欠条金额；Josef把数字写在手上，伸过去给Anna Kral 2看。
  _effects: condition; flag +legal_aid_file_lost; knowledge “the clinic lost the intake card between visits”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Friedrich Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Friedrich Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。

  Josef和Anton Stein在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Anton Stein在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000687:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

  _effects: cash 9 (沙龙赞助人的支票); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +paintings_used_to_hide_damp; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; knowledge “the paintings cover damp patches in the corridor”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

  _effects: cash -0.2 (次日再跑一趟所需的电车票); skill_

  Josef看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Elisabeth Kral 2追出来问怎么不领了，Josef说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Josef把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; food_

  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000687:child; mood -0.010000000000000231_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  _effects: cash -8 (遗产清单上的犁); flag +appealed_inheritance_tax_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “the village records are locked in the auditor’s own cabinet”_

  分房名单又往后推了一年，Josef一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Rosa Huber 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Josef被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

- **1952.04** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef对Emil Stein说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Emil Stein把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  Josef没有争，把遗嘱交给Friedrich Moser存档，自己只拿了父亲的一只烟斗。Anna Bauer后来把房子卖了，钱分了一半送来。Friedrich Moser 2说这院子以后没梨吃了；Josef把烟斗点上，说树还在别人家院子里。
  _effects: cash 8 (遗嘱第三页被水泡过); flag +settled_inheritance_without_dispute; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; knowledge “the house was sold and the proceeds split in half”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Anton Stein把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。

  Josef和Mitzi Berg买了影戏棚最便宜的边座，边座和两粒糖一共五十赫勒。一刻钟的活动影戏里火车朝观众冲过来，棚里有人叫出了声。散场后两人还站在门口看下一场的招牌，Josef把剩下几个铜板在口袋里攥得发热，又买了两粒糖，一人一粒含回家。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (影戏棚边座和两粒糖); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1953.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Josef当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001962:child_

- **1953.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Josef额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Anna Winter坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1953.10** `r3-war.stretcher-bearer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::ambulance-train/forced-wounded-onto-train` tags: war, army, stretcher, rescue, medicine, vienna, urban, postwar
  站台上有个兵不肯上车，说要回前线找兄弟。Josef把担架横在车门前，Otto Moser 2从后面抱住那人。那人挣扎时踢翻了热水桶，Josef的裤腿烫掉一层皮，还是把人推上了车。
  _effects: condition; flag +forced_wounded_onto_train; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/settle-at-the-tavern` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  没有签字，陪对方去酒馆把账当面谈清。掌柜的把欠条一张张摆在桌上，一共二十八克朗。有人替着说了几句好话，讲明利息怎么算、按月还多少，请掌柜的宽限到月底。谈下来先还五克朗，余下的立了新据，担保人一栏空着，掌柜的临走时瞪了一眼。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (酒馆里替对方先垫的第一笔款子和两杯酒); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000559:child; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Josef听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Anna Winter扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.08** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Otto Stein在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Otto Stein在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000444:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1955.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/stamp-on-credit` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Huber把挂号纸推回来，说没有疾病保险的印花就不能看门诊。Josef数出几枚硬币，手在柜台上停住，后面的人绕过他往前挤，把窗口堵得更紧。Luise Huber最后把印花本翻到最后一页，说先记在账上，月底一起收。
  _effects: cash -2 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); flag +clinic_fee_recorded_as_debt; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003104:child; knowledge “the outpatient fee can be recorded against next month’s wage”_

- **1955.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000444:child; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租)_

- **1955.10** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/took-the-room` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand Huber 2趁Josef不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Josef回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Ferdinand Huber 2站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000007; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:457_

- **1956.01** `fm17-seance.parlor-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/a-name-she-could-not-know` tags: faith, ritual, seance, grief, spiritualism, money, doubt, vienna
  Rosa Stein把窗帘拉上，让Josef把手放在桌面上。铜盘轻轻响了一声，Rosa Stein说出了一个只有Josef和死者知道的小名，还提到葬礼那天落在棺盖上的一枚铜纽扣。Josef没有哭，只把手指从桌面上收回来，握住了自己的袖口。
  _effects: cash -2 (降神会的场地费与蜡烛钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001314:child; knowledge “降神会说出了只有我和死者知道的一件小事。”; flag +fm17_seance_comforted; state_add fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:472; flag +fm17_cult_joined_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/let-them-talk` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天下午窗边三把椅子，介绍只用了两句，随后就任两人自己聊。老友讲印厂的油墨味，缝纫女工讲机针怎么断。有人只管叫侍者续咖啡，三杯一共九十赫勒，坐在旁边听了一下午。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和给侍者的小费); skill; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1956.04** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Otto Weiss的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Josef在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child_

- **1956.05** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Berta Kral在午休时把本子摊在Josef的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Josef从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Berta Kral当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006701:child; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

- **1956.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Anna Stein把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition_

- **1957.01** `fm17-cult.exit-cost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/stays-for-the-child` tags: faith, cult, exit, family, child, money, belonging, vienna
  Josef已经把行李收到门口，Hermann Bauer却从院里跑回来，说学堂里有人答应教他写字，还说外面的孩子会笑他没有父亲。Josef在门槛上坐了很久，最后把行李又提回屋里。第二天，Josef照常去后厅摆椅子。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 40 -> 50; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 20 -> 35; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 25 -> 35; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +fm17_cult_stayed_for_child_

- **1957.01** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  Otto Stein从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Josef买双能进厂的结实靴子。Josef数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); health 0.019999999999999997 (脚上有了不漏水的靴子); relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000444:child_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:fm17-cult-initiate/the-circle-year/keeps-asking` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  又过了一年，Josef仍坐在那间借来的后厅里，但开始记下每次募捐的数目。会长说怀疑也是修行的一部分；Josef把这句话抄在本子上，旁边画了一个问号。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 35 -> 43; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.belonging 55 -> 57; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Anna Winter找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.03** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Rosa Stein低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001314:child; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

  站台上有个兵不肯上车，说要回前线找兄弟。Josef把担架横在车门前，Gustav Moser 2从后面抱住那人。那人挣扎时踢翻了热水桶，Josef的裤腿烫掉一层皮，还是把人推上了车。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

  Ferdinand Huber 2带来一锅还热着的扁豆汤，没说要祈祷，也没说死者去了哪里。两个人坐在厨房里，一个念珠，一个喝汤；念到第三遍时，Josef把碗放下，说了一句只有自己听得见的话。Ferdinand Huber 2没有追问，只把面包往她那边推了推。
  _effects: food; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; flag +fm17_consolation_without_miracle_

  Josef在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。

  Gustav Moser 2让Josef咬住一块木楔，把钳子伸进嘴里，脚蹬着椅背使劲。牙根断在牙龈里，Gustav Moser 2又换了一把小凿子。出门时Josef的半个脸已经肿起来，雪落在脸上，反而觉得凉得舒服。

  Grete Huber把Josef拦在门口，说病人刚打过针，不能见客。Josef把糖水梨留在护士台，嘱咐护士转交；走到楼梯口又回来，问病人有没有说想吃什么。护士翻了一眼记录，说只说过想喝凉水。
  _effects: cash -1 (病房里的糖水梨); flag +hospital_visit_refused; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004595:child; knowledge “the friend asked only for cold water”_

- **1959.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Josef走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个)_

- **1959.06** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Josef和Otto Stein在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Josef母亲上个月的药钱。Otto Stein把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000444:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  两赫勒从内袋滚到Josef掌心，铜的凉意。他把那册书夹在腋下，和Elisabeth Huber一起走下台阶，管理员在锁门前看了眼他的借书条，什么也没多问。
  _effects: skill; cash -0.02 (向管理员缴纳的借书押金); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004967:child_

- **1960.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  疗养院的护士接过行李，把床号写在小黑板上。Josef躺下时听见走廊尽头有人在放唱片，咳嗽没那么紧了。安娜的信第三天到，说花浇过了，房东没来催租。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000559:child_

- **1961.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/takes-the-train` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Bauer把行李捆在自行车后座上，说要去城里找活。Josef往包里塞了两个面包和一双旧袜子，没有送到车站；火车开走以后，Josef才发现自己一直站在原地数车厢。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> true; flag +child_left_home; cash 1 (one fewer mouth at the table); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1961.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，他把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Josef又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1961.05** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/understudy-contract` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth 2演主角，Josef拿到的是替角合同：国王的戏服先按主角的尺寸做，Josef只能在排练时穿。导演说这不是坏事，至少不用负责谢幕。首演夜，Josef坐在后台，听见前台每一次掌声都像在敲自己的门。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:562; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +understudy_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1961.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Josef摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Ferdinand Huber 2第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Josef只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Josef替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Josef自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -2 (coffee and entry); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

  _effects: state_add chronic-illness state:world-vienna-1912:580; cash -7 (specialist consultation and a second test); knowledge “专科医生说这病不会很快好，只会慢慢改变能干多少活。”_

- **1916.03** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Rosa把木马推给弟妹，说“这个给你”，自己翻开那本旧图画书。纸页发黄，角上留着别人的铅笔字。Alois Lechner问真的不要木马吗，Rosa点头，眼睛没离开书页。此后几天，Rosa一有空就翻那本书，连书脊都摸得发亮。

- **1916.12** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-hides-bread` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  锅里只剩一层薄汤。Rosa趁Alois Lechner去院里提水，掰下半个面包塞进嘴里，又把剩下的藏进围裙口袋。晚饭时Alois Lechner数了面包，没有骂，只把Rosa的那份挪到自己盘边。夜里Rosa听见灶台那边有轻轻的哭声。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +hid_bread; mood -0.05_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care

  _effects: health -0.03 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000077:child_

- **1918.06** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors

  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; education apprenticeship_

  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-theresia-huber; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  _effects: cash -6 (夜里敲接生婆的门（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +rural_cart_fetched_midwife; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  _effects: food; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1931.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood -0.05_

  棉纱缠了三圈，橙色碎屑沾在布面上。Rosa用左手推料，右手悬着不敢使劲，每压一下冲床，指根就跳一下。工头走过来瞥了一眼，说这才像干活的样子。
  _effects: condition; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Berta Hofer嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Berta Hofer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Berta Hofer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  Rosa和Elisabeth Bauer在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Elisabeth Bauer把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Rosa把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Elisabeth Bauer把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1935.01** `r13-fam.invite-cafe::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::cafe-war/ersatz-coffee` tags: task, social, cafe, friendship, invitation, vienna, urban, crisis
  咖啡馆已经改成配给站，Rosa把Gustav Huber 2请回自家厨房，用炒过的大麦煮了一壶。两人围着炉子，把糖票摊在桌上算来算去；Gustav Huber 2说这味道像战前的真咖啡，Rosa没有拆穿，只又添了一勺。
  _effects: cash -1 (靠窗那张小桌); mood 0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +shared_ersatz_coffee; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1935.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Rosa没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000385:child_

- **1936.01** `r1-civ.return-from-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reentered-under-amnesty` tags: migration, exile, return, papers, home, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Huber 2在边境检查站翻Rosa的护照，说上面的名字和档案里的不一样，要等总部回电。Rosa在长椅上坐了一夜，听见隔壁房间有人在唱一首自己也会唱的歌。天亮时，Ferdinand Huber 2把护照还回来，说“可以进，但先去警察局报到”。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +reentered_country_under_amnesty; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the border checks the old name against the political file”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1936.01** `r2-law.inheritance-tax-audit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/appealed-assessment` tags: law, inheritance, tax, audit, farm, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa对估价不服，找Gustav Huber 2写申诉，说母牛去年就死了。Rosa Winter要求死牛证明；兽医的证明在村里，村长说档案柜的钥匙在Rosa Winter手里。Rosa跑了两趟，最后把申诉和一张村长签字的纸条一起递上去；Rosa Winter收下，说等三个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (遗产清单上的犁（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +appealed_inheritance_tax_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the village records are locked in the auditor’s own cabinet”_

- **1936.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-as-hand` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  铁门只开了一条缝，Otto Bauer把人数到一百二十，才放最前面十个进去。Rosa把冻裂的手摊在灯下，指节上的茧替她答了话。Otto Bauer在纸角写了个“明早六点”，Friedrich Stein 2被推到一边，脚边的麻袋口还开着，土豆滚进了排水沟。
  _effects: cash 2 (铁门开了一条缝); flag +work13_hired_at_factory_gate; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; employment Tagelöhner @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1936.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Friedrich Moser从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child_

- **1936.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  抽屉一推就卡住，Rosa用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1937.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Friedrich Moser嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Friedrich Moser家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Friedrich Moser那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006261_

  _effects: cash -2 (请假条上的墨点（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +sick_leave_granted_with_deduction; knowledge “the workshop deducts half pay for sick days”_

  Rosa在更衣室门口对Luise Weiss说，名额让给家里有病人的工友，自己家里还撑得住。工头盯着他看了几秒，把名字划掉又重新写。那人的夜班费有了着落；过了几天，Luise Weiss把一批轻活的单子派给了Rosa，说这活不累，钱不少。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (让出的加班费); reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1937.05** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-report` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  样张放在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的吸墨纸上，对方只说了一句知道了。Alois Lechner从办公室出来时脸是白的，走过字盘时肩膀撞了Rosa一下。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; cash 1.5 (雇主给的赏钱); reputation -0.15000000000000002_

- **1937.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Rosa把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-elisabeth-bauer; skill_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Rosa在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Rosa Winter记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Rosa带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa带着Grete Huber去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Grete Huber一直捏着那张课程表，Rosa则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 65 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa把借方和贷方弄反，试算表怎么也不平。Rosa熬到半夜，把整本账重抄一遍，第二天交给Berta Winter时，还是错了一笔。Berta Winter让Rosa从第一页重新学起，Rosa把错的那页折起来，没舍得撕。Berta Winter把自己的旧账本借给Rosa，让他从头再抄。这次没有错。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; skill_

- **1938.06** `kernel-civic.leaflet-night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/civic-leaflet-print` tags: politics, leaflet, print, repression, organization, vienna, urban, crisis
  印刷机停下来的间隙，Gustav Kral把一叠还温热的传单推到她面前，让她按街区折成三摞。传单上只印着明天的集会和一句“带上你的工牌”，没有署名；她用围裙擦掉指尖的油墨，把最上面一张塞进内袋。
  _effects: flag +printed_leaflet; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; cash -1 (买纸和补铅字的钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “传单只写集会和工牌，不写组织者姓名，是为了让散发的人少担风险”_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; reputation -0.019999999999999962_

  _effects: cash 1 (罢工的第七天); reputation 0.03999999999999998; state_remove strike-wave state:world-vienna-1912:201_

  _effects: cash 15 (第三个栏架前的马); mood 0.2; condition; flag +gambling_winnings; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  _effects: cash 2 (铜板落进琴盒); health 0.019999999999999997 (铜板落进琴盒); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +invited_indoors; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  _effects: condition; food_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

  _effects: state_add strike-committee state:world-vienna-1912:241; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +joined_strike_committee_

  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Rosa Moser 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Rosa Moser 2敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1941.11** `r2-law.fire-insurance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/kerosene-exclusion` tags: finance, insurance, fire, contract, housing, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Kral来收保费时，Rosa正在修煤油炉。Gustav Kral把保单翻到背面，指着一条小字说，明火取暖不在承保范围。Rosa问那冬天怎么办，Gustav Kral说可以加保，也可以把炉子搬到院子里；Ferdinand Bauer在隔壁听见，笑了一声。
  _effects: cash -3 (保单背面的小字（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +fire_policy_excludes_kerosene_stove; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; knowledge “the fire policy excludes open-flame heating”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1942.01** `hist15-occ.work-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/permit-granted` tags: occupation, work, permit, institution, shortage, history, vienna, urban
  Friedrich Moser在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Rosa拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Rosa把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1942.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.refused` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Rosa算了算家里的粮和煤，说实在住不下。教父Hermann Weiss在车站的长椅上睡了两夜，后来被巡夜的人带去收容所；Rosa听说后，托人送去一床旧毯子，没有留名字。收容所的回执上只写了姓名和日期。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; role; role; cash -1 (an old blanket sent to the shelter); flag +relative_turned_away; knowledge “亲戚在车站长椅上睡了两夜，后来被巡夜的人带走。”_

- **1942.05** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/moves-the-bed-in` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Rosa Lechner在楼梯口喘了两次才上到三楼。Rosa没有开会，也没有和谁商量，晚上就把折叠床搬进走廊尽头的小房间，把闹钟拨到凌晨两点。第二天，Rosa在药盒上贴了第一张写着时间的纸。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:253; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +moved_parent_into_the_flat_

- **1942.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa没有立刻答话，先去敲Alois Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Alois Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Rosa把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1942.07** `r3-war.demobilization::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop::back-at-the-factory/inherited-dead-machinists-caliper` tags: war, demobilization, veteran, factory, postwar, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa回厂第一天，发现车床换了新皮带，原来的老师傅死在战壕里。Katharina Hofer让Rosa带两个学徒，Rosa说“我自己还没摸熟”；Katharina Hofer说“你打过仗，学得快”。午休时，Rosa在更衣箱里找到老师傅留下的卡尺。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +inherited_dead_machinists_caliper; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1943.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/wrong-register` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  登记处的老职员把Grete Huber的名字写成了Rosa小时候的名字。Rosa指出错误，对方却说“你们家的人长得都一样”；Grete Huber拿着写错名字的课程表回家，一路上都在偷笑。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 32 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 51 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 65 -> 58; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 2; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; flag +wrong_school_register_

- **1943.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa跑了一趟医院，把Alois Lechner送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Rosa回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Rosa又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；{patient}今年记得住Rosa的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 30 -> 26; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 10 -> 8; health -0.010000000000000002 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `r13-edu.teach-younger-worker::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/trained-apprentice` tags: education, teaching, apprentice, work, craft, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa让Maria Winter先看自己做了三遍，再把锉刀交过去。Maria Winter第一下就锉歪了，Rosa没有抢回来，只让Maria Winter自己量。一个月后，Maria Winter能独立做简单的键，Rosa在工头面前替Maria Winter说了句话，工头把Maria Winter的名字写进正式名单。Maria Winter第一次独立上工，Rosa站在旁边没插手。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +trained_apprentice; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001912:child_

- **1943.10** `r15-org.workshop-closure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/wages-cut` tags: work, factory, organization, unemployment, collective, vienna, urban, crisis
  厂子没有关，但工钱减了三成，工时加了一个钟头。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说这是暂时的。没有人相信“暂时”，但也没有人当场走。
  _effects: cash -2 (车间门口的封条（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; state_add strike-wave state:world-vienna-1912:267_

- **1943.12** `r14-soc.courtyard-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/opened-the-flat-door` tags: rumor, reputation, neighbor_conflict, shame, gender, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把Ferdinand Stein请上楼，推开自己那间屋的门，让对方面对一张空床、一只药罐和母亲的遗像。Ferdinand Stein在门口站了一会儿，说“我也是听来的”。水烧开了，两人谁也没有去提壶。临走时Ferdinand Stein把门带上，动作很轻；第二天，洗衣房里没有人再提那件事。茶壶在炉子上叫了很久，最后自己凉了。
  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +disarmed_rumor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1944.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009704_

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/public-suspicion` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  楼里的邻居开始议论Rosa和Rosa Kral 2。有人把门牌记下来，房东来敲门，说这栋楼不接受“这种住法”；Rosa Kral 2把箱子拖出来又推回床下，搬家花掉五克朗，两人那一夜都没睡稳。Rosa在院子里被人盯着看，门上的名字也被人用手指划过。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; reputation -0.12; mood -0.09999999999999998; cash -5 (moving and lost rent（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费（其中2克朗记入债务）); knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  {patient}今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Rosa在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 26 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 8 -> 14; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1944.01** `r13-edu.workers-college::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory::factory-sponsored-college/weekend-college-place` tags: education, workers_college, adult_education, union, essay, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa的申请被厂方压住，说车间离不开人。Rosa没有辞职，把课程表抄在工具箱内侧，每天午休时自学一小时。半年后，Luise Stein 2来厂里回访，看见Rosa的笔记，替Rosa争取到一个周末班名额。周末班的教室在城另一头，他每周走四十分钟。
  _effects: flag +weekend_college_place; skill_

- **1944.02** `r3-crime.police-raid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::political-week/lined-against-the-wall` tags: crime, police, tenement, fear, politics, vienna, urban, crisis
  门是被靴子踹开的，Ferdinand Huber 2把整层楼的人赶到走廊，让他们面朝墙站好，说“谁动谁先进去”。Otto Weiss的睡帽掉在地上，Maria Winter只穿着袜子。抽屉被倒空，床垫被划开，Rosa攒了三个月的房租从茶叶罐里消失，没有人说要开收据。
  _effects: cash -7 (凌晨四点门闩被撬开（其中7克朗记入债务）); mood -0.17999999999999994; condition; flag +survived_police_raid; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the raid team took cash without logging it”_

  Rosa停机等送货车，把别的活往后推。车到傍晚才来，剧院主顾已经走了。Katharina Hofer在误工单上记了一笔，这个月的加班名单上没了影。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; cash -0.6 (误工少算的工钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Rosa看Alois Lechner盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Alois Lechner愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Rosa门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Rosa打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。

  Rosa的手指在字母盘上找键，找了三个星期才不用低头。结业测试要求每分钟四十个词，Rosa打到第三十九个时卡住，深吸一口气，把最后一行打完。Maria Stein掐停秒表，说可以去找工作了，Rosa的手还在抖。Maria Stein把秒表收进抽屉。

  车夫把价钱咬得很死，Rosa还到五克朗又添了二十赫勒，才说定跑一趟。Josef扶着床架，车过路口时煤箱歪了一下，细灰撒在路面上。到了地方，东西卸在门厅，天已经大亮。

  {patient}今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Rosa在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。

- **1947.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/work-debt-new-loan` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa还不上旧账，只好向Alois Bauer介绍的放贷人再借十克朗，月息两成。Alois Weiss收了旧账，又重新开了一页赊账；Rosa在两本账上都按了手印。回家路上，Rosa把两张借据分开藏，怕家里人看见。
  _effects: cash 10 (借新债还旧账); flag +new_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “放贷人的月息是两成，借据要分开藏。”; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1947.06** `r3-war.demobilization::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop::back-at-the-factory/inherited-dead-machinists-caliper` tags: war, demobilization, veteran, factory, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  Rosa回厂第一天，发现车床换了新皮带，原来的老师傅死在战壕里。Grete Huber 2让Rosa带两个学徒，Rosa说“我自己还没摸熟”；Grete Huber 2说“你打过仗，学得快”。午休时，Rosa在更衣箱里找到老师傅留下的卡尺。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999796; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1947.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.school-fork/apprentice-at-printer` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Rosa在印刷厂的招工窗口替孩子报了学徒，第一年每周带回两克朗工钱。Anna Winter在成绩单背面写的推荐话被折进信封，和学徒契约一起锁在抽屉里。孩子第一天回来，袖口沾着油墨，说师傅让先搬纸；Rosa数着那两克朗，没再提升学的事。
  _effects: cash 2 (孩子学徒第一周带回工钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000258:child; flag +child_apprentice_printer_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Rosa一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Katharina 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002446:child_

- **1948.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/stays-for-now` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, postwar
  Grete Huber说再等一年，等家里的债还掉一点。Rosa没有劝，只把晚饭多添了一勺；夜里Rosa听见Grete Huber在隔壁翻书，煤油灯一直亮到后半夜。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 42; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 39 -> 43; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_expire:lc17-caregiver` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Rosa的「照料一个衰弱的亲人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:253_

- **1948.01** `r13-work.rebuild-after-fire::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/only-boarded-up` tags: commerce, fire, rebuilding, shop, debt, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa算了一遍，重铺屋顶的钱够租两年新铺面，于是只把临街的窗用木板钉上。货搬到后院，熟客从侧门进来，买完就走。Grete Huber 2来看了两次，说这样下去租约到期就不续；Rosa把钉子又敲紧了一颗。
  _effects: cash -4 (烧黑的房梁); mood -0.010000000000000009; condition; flag +work13_shop_boarded_up_after_fire_

- **1948.04** `major-crisis.press-accident/light-duty` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, employment
  离合器救下了Rosa的大半只手，但左手的两根指头被压扁，骨头长好以后也弯不回来。工厂把Rosa调到校对台，工资降到八十克朗；事故保险只认“部分丧失劳动能力”，赔了十二克朗。Grete Huber 2替Rosa把工具柜搬下楼，谁都没提那台机器为什么没有护罩。
  _effects: condition; employment Buchdrucker (Schonposten) @ org-winter-print; cash 12 (partial disability payment from the accident insurer); role; flag +light_duty; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement_

- **1948.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工资袋上的油墨还没干透，Rosa的手指染了一点黑。母亲把硬币倒在厨房桌上，一枚枚排开，排到第六枚时停住。灶上的水壶响了，她拿起来又放下，没继续问。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

  Rosa把十二只木箱码在板车上，用麻绳捆了两道，轮子碾过石子路时，箱角互相磕着响。走到第三道街口，车轴响了一声，Alois Weiss蹲下去紧了紧销子。货送到时比约定早半个钟头，店主验完封条，在送货单上画了押。
  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1949.03** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Karl Kraus用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +parish_registered_

- **1949.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.work-injury/rag-and-work` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  棉纱缠了三圈，橙色碎屑沾在布面上。Rosa用左手推料，右手悬着不敢使劲，每压一下冲床，指根就跳一下。工头走过来瞥了一眼，说这才像干活的样子。
  _effects: health -0.06 (手指伤口未妥善处理); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1949.10** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Rosa眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Anna Winter隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); health -0.010000000000000002 (生火时被烟呛到); food_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012582_

- **1950.01** `r14-soc.repair-fence::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/shared-the-mason` tags: task, neighbor_conflict, repair, boundary, reconciliation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa请来上次那个泥瓦匠，把界墙重新砌直，灰浆里掺了新石灰。Katharina Hofer在墙那边递砖，手上沾着白灰，递到第三块时说了句“上次是我急”。墙砌到齐腰高，两家的孩子已经趴在上面看蚂蚁。收工时，Katharina Hofer把剩下的半袋石灰留在Rosa家门口，没有留字条。
  _effects: cash -6 (界墙上的新灰浆); mood 0.12; flag +rebuilt_boundary_wall_

- **1950.03** `vienna-women-housework.care-old-parent/care.hire-neighbor` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  上午的日头照进走廊，Josef Winter扶着Rosa Lechner慢慢挪到椅子上。Rosa听着隔壁的动静，低头缝完一整排扣眼，抬头时发现汤锅已经坐在炉上，火不旺不弱。
  _effects: cash -2.4 (付给邻居半个月的搭手钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood 0.020000000000000018; health -0.010000000000000009 (夜里仍需起身照料，疲劳未消)_

- **1950.04** `p9-work-printshop.type-case/patch-smaller-type` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, typesetting, rush_order
  Rosa用小一号的 e 填满空位，远处看还齐整，近看行距发虚。Katharina Hofer 3翻了两页，扣了半天工钱，一克朗二十赫勒，说下回自己想法子。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (字距不齐被扣的半天工钱); skill_

- **1950.04** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-print-secretly` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Emil Bauer趁夜班把一版工会呼吁加薪的传单塞进Rosa的排字盘里，说是替工友印的，谁都不会知道。Rosa把那张纸压到排版最底层，机器照样轰隆转。
  _effects: flag +printed_union_pamphlet; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.08** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.refused` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的走廊里拦住Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne，话还没说完，对方就摆手说账上没这笔规矩。Rosa回车间时，工头正往布告栏上钉新的计件单。
  _effects: reputation -0.050000000000000044_

  Franz Huber上错了火车，半夜又提着别人的箱子回来。箱子里是一套戏服和一顶礼帽；Rosa没有骂，只把箱子送到车站，回来时Franz Huber已经趴在桌上睡着了。
  _effects: flag +wrong_train_suitcase; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 55; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Rosa的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Rosa被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.030000000000000027 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”_

- **1951.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  楼道里正晾着几家洗过的衣服，Rosa和Elisabeth Kral 2站在水滴下面说话，声音压得低。Elisabeth Kral 2说这栋楼里的住户不是头一回被这样要钱，先凑个口信比一个人硬顶强。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1951.09** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Rosa托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Rosa Lechner擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Rosa晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Rosa Lechner的腿伤有人料理，Rosa却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用); health 0.08000000000000002 (看护妇料理后伤势好转); health -0.020000000000000018 (夜间加班的疲劳)_

- **1952.04** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa端着一只旧锅去敲Grete Huber的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Grete Huber让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Rosa连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在更衣室门口对Luise Hofer 2说，名额让给家里有病人的工友，自己家里还撑得住。工头盯着他看了几秒，把名字划掉又重新写。那人的夜班费有了着落；过了几天，Luise Hofer 2把一批轻活的单子派给了Rosa，说这活不累，钱不少。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (让出的加班费); reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

  _effects: cash -1 (拒签后被压减的计件活); skill_

  夜校的名额满了，登记的人让下个季度再来。Alois Lechner把报名表折好，说本来也不是非学不可；Rosa看见Alois Lechner把那张纸夹进了祈祷书。回家的电车上，Alois Lechner一直看着窗外的招牌，忽然问Rosa那个字念什么。

  Luise Huber把Rosa带到车站，给了一张去乡下的车票，说案子结之前别回来。Rosa只带了一个布包，Gustav Stein站在月台尽头，没有靠近。火车开动时，Rosa看见他抬手比了一个割喉的动作，怕她忘了。
  _effects: condition; flag +witness_moved_for_protection; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003104:child; knowledge “the brother knows the train route”_

  Alois Lechner敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Rosa那天刚和Alois Lechner为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Alois Lechner抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Alois Lechner低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。

  Rosa在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Anna Winter把Rosa的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Rosa把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Rosa在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1954.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Franz Winter追出来问怎么不领了，Rosa说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Rosa把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; food_

- **1954.06** `vienna-printshop-daily.overtime-order/overtime-accept` tags: work, factory, labour, social, injury
  油灯下Emil Stein递过来一块干面包，Rosa一边啃一边把纸喂进压印机。天蒙蒙亮时Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne数出几枚硬币拍在桌上，没多一句话。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (通宵加班费); health -0.050000000000000044 (通宵站立和油墨气味); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1954.08** `r2-law.life-insurance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory::r2-law.life-insurance-reform/kept-old-card` tags: finance, insurance, health, premium, family, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa没有转会，仍旧把硬币交给Rosa Hofer 2。三个月后，Rosa Hofer 2说公司被互助会并了，旧卡只能按半价转。Rosa把卡翻来覆去看，打孔的地方已经连成一条线。Rosa Kral 2说早该换，Rosa说换不换都一样。
  _effects: cash -4 (每周二十赫勒的死亡); flag +kept_lapsed_industrial_policy; knowledge “the old policy transferred at half value after the merger”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1955.01** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-argue-and-win` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Emil Stein和另外两个人跟着Rosa去了办公室门口。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne最后从抽屉里摸出几枚硬币，边给边在本子上写了一行字。
  _effects: cash 1 (退还的部分扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015359_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.mentor-worker::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/youth-reported-him` tags: work, mentoring, safety, solidarity, youth, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa提醒Elisabeth Huber别替Rosa Hofer 2私下搬料，Elisabeth Huber却把这话学给了Rosa Hofer 2听。下午Rosa Hofer 2把Rosa叫到办公室，问是不是在拉帮结派。Rosa说是教新人规矩，Rosa Hofer 2让他少管闲事；走出办公室，Elisabeth Huber正在擦机器，头也没抬。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999898; flag +work13_mentoring_reported; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004967:child_

- **1955.02** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/print-at-night` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Rosa收下Emil Lechner 4递来的一把钥匙，夜里开机印完三百份。Mitzi Stein在三点前取走，纸钱压在台灯下，废页和原稿一起塞进炉子，火苗发蓝。
  _effects: cash 2 (夜里印传单分到的纸钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (熬了一整夜印活); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000559:child_

- **1955.03** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1955.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1955.07** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Josef Hofer听，Josef Hofer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1955.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Josef Moser 3说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Josef Moser 3把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162_

- **1955.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1956.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015937_

- **1956.01** `task-vienna-medical-work.poor-patient/poor-patient-refused` tags: health, medicine, hospital, care, knowledge, profession, task, task:health
  Rosa想起这家上次的诊费也没结，把处方推回去，只留下那张饮食单，说先照这个吃，药缓缓。Franz Winter 2把硬币收回口袋，站在药房门口没走，最后把饮食单折成小方块捏在手心，回去的路上下起了雨。
  _effects: health -0.03 (没有拿到药)_

- **1956.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Rosa用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Berta Kral翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Hedwig在走廊里问交了多少，Rosa含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.10** `r1-cul.spiritualist-seance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/table-rapped` tags: faith, seance, grief, fraud, occult, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa在烛光里握着手，桌子敲了三下。Anna Bauer闭着眼说，是一个穿军装的人。Emil Stein在角落里冷笑，伸手掀开桌布，下面只有一根细线；Anna Bauer没有睁眼，说线是上一个客人留下的。
  _effects: cash -2 (桌子敲了三下); flag +attended_seance; knowledge “a thin thread runs under the seance table”_

- **1956.11** `p9-work-printshop.type-case/apprentice-crawl` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, typesetting, rush_order
  Rosa让Berta Kral趴在地上按字母把撒掉的铅字捡回格子，自己先排标题。活拖到下午三点才交，主顾骂了几句，学徒膝盖上全是灰。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006701:child; skill_

- **1956.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Rosa没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa把Grete 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/minder-cancels` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Bauer 3说自家孙子也病了，这星期看不了。Rosa抱着Grete 2站在楼道里，听见厂里的汽笛响过第一遍。那天Rosa没有去上工，第二天门口的通知上多了一个红叉；Mitzi Bauer 3端来一碗粥，说下星期一定帮忙。
  _effects: cash -1 (上工前把小的送下楼); mood -0.09000000000000008; condition; flag +missed_work_for_childcare; knowledge “a missed shift is marked with a red cross”_

- **1957.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria 2这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 18; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Elisabeth Kral 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Elisabeth Kral 2陪着Rosa去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1957.02** `vienna-adult-social.wage-loan/wage-loan.granted` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的账房里向Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne开口预支十二克朗，因为家里的煤和面包钱同时到期。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从抽屉里数出钞票，让会计在发薪日扣回，并记下“下不为例”。
  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +stopped_taking_communion; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; knowledge “the funeral organist played the whole piece without a break”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  Rosa把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Katharina Hofer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1958.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Anton Kral却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +still_looking_for_room_

  负责人说这是不信任，Rosa回说数目对得上才叫信任。Ferdinand Huber 2把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill_

  Ferdinand Huber 2派了一名检查员去看房，回来说厕所漏水、电线裸露，不适合居住。Otto Weiss说可以降租，Ferdinand Huber 2说降租也要先修。Rosa问修房期间住哪里，Ferdinand Huber 2说可以申请临时安置；安置名单排到明年，Josef Hofer说先住我家阁楼。
  _effects: cash -4 (驱逐听证的十五分钟); condition; flag +eviction_case_referred_to_housing_inspection; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; knowledge “the temporary housing list runs into next year”_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa天没亮就把Maria 2裹好，抱到楼下Ferdinand Huber 2家。Ferdinand Huber 2接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Rosa看灶上温着的米汤。Rosa把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Ferdinand Huber 2数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Rosa听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Rosa把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1959.03** `r1-fam.family-shame::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/shame.hidden` tags: family, shame, union, embezzlement, loyalty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa和Anna Stein一起把这件事瞒了下来。他们对外说账本是雨水泡坏的，又凑钱补上了亏空。可走廊里的Elisabeth Huber早就看见Anna Stein半夜从工会后门出来；风言风语传了半年，Rosa的母亲在市场上跟人吵了一架，回来病了一场。
  _effects: cash -10 (工会账本上的空行); condition; flag +covered_the_union_deficit_quietly; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004967:child; knowledge “邻居看见弟弟半夜从工会后门出来。”_

- **1959.11** `p9-history-union-political.meeting/meeting-speak-up` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Rosa站起来报出自己车间的计件工价：糊一百个纸盒二十八赫勒，比隔壁车间少六个。屋里一下子静了，Emil Bauer把草稿推过来，让书记员把这几个数字写在动议第一行。散会时有人拍了拍Rosa的肩膀。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1960.01** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Alois Kral 3把Rosa寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Rosa追到码头时，Alois Kral 3已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Rosa在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Maria 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

  午休时Katharina Stein坐在对面，Rosa看见Rosa的手腕上有一道新划痕，想问，又觉得不关自己的事。Josef Moser 3来催下午的活，Katharina Stein把饭盒一收就进了车间。Rosa后来听说Katharina Stein那天下午被皮带擦伤了胳膊，伤得不重，但请了三天假。
  _effects: flag +work13_stayed_silent_with_young_worker; knowledge “the young worker was injured at the belt a week later”_

- **1960.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Rosa把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Rosa Moser站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Rosa手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); reputation 0.03999999999999998_
