  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (补完老人剩下的半小时); skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-162_

- **1962.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa没有立刻答话，先去敲Anton Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anton Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Rosa把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1962.11** `kernel-health.workplace-injury::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/work-injury-fatal` tags: health, work, injury, factory, disability, vienna, urban, postwar
  夜班的传动带没有护罩。Rosa的袖子被卷进去，整个人被拖向齿轮箱；Helene Hofer 2拉下总闸时，机器还在转。工头下午就叫人装了铁栏，订单没有停。订单照旧，夜班照旧，门房换了新的登记簿。没人再提护罩。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in an unguarded drive belt); cash 25 (factory death benefit and accident insurance)_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Emil分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Emil的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling

  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1926.08** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Emil的手，又让Emil推两下刨子试试。Emil用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josef Bauer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.09** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Luise Stein 2把成绩单按在讲台上，说Emil还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Emil签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

  Ferdinand Stein在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Emil下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; health -0.030000000000000027 (白天上工夜里上课睡眠不足)_

  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1932.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Rosa Winter忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Emil陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Rosa Winter站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1932.06** `p9-history-union-political.split/split-open-debate` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  Emil把两派的名单都推到桌子中间，说先别表决，把分歧印出来公开辩论，会费和会员证经不起一次分裂。Alois Weiss皱眉想了半天，最后同意下周再议，会费簿子当场传了一圈。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-passed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Otto Weiss从考场出来，手里攥着准考证，说最后一道题做出来了。放榜那天，Emil陪Otto Weiss去看榜，名字在中间偏下，够了。Otto Weiss跳起来，又立刻站好，回头问Emil能不能把旧课本留下。报名费两克朗，Emil替Otto Weiss交了。
  _effects: cash -2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_passed_exam_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002938_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Kral 2的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Mitzi Berg扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Emil把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”_

- **1933.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil看Gustav Berg 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Gustav Berg 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Emil门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Emil打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; food_

- **1933.05** `kernel-social.misunderstanding-apology::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/misunderstanding.jam-and-apology` tags: social, misunderstanding, apology, friendship, neighbor, reputation, reform, medical
  话是从洗衣房传出来的：有人说Emil笑过Karl Kraus死去的孩子。Emil提着一罐李子酱上楼，在门口把事情从头说了一遍，Karl Kraus听完把酱收下，又拿出两只杯子。两人坐到天黑，谁也没再提那句话，只商量明天的煤该谁去买。
  _effects: relation friend with person-karl-kraus; role; cash -1 (a jar of plum jam for the apology（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +friendship_repaired_

- **1933.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Emil走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个（其中0.08克朗记入债务）); health -0.010000000000000009 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

- **1934.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Elisabeth Kral 2在几天后找到Emil，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; household; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004975_

- **1934.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004977_

- **1934.01** `r14-soc.gamble-away-wages::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/won-fifteen` tags: task, gambling_debt, gambling, money, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil押的那匹马在最后一个弯道超了过去。Maria Hofer 2把十五克朗拍在柜台上，说“运气不错”。Emil请Gustav Huber 2喝了半升啤酒，剩下的钱在口袋里响了一路。第二天，Emil又去了赛马场。那匹马的号码被Emil写在烟盒上，后来烟盒被雨淋烂了，号码还认得出。回家后，Emil把那张湿透的烟盒摊在炉边烘干。
  _effects: cash 15 (第三个栏架前的马); mood 0.19999999999999996; condition; flag +gambling_winnings; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Emil把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005629_

- **1934.06** `r1-fam.friendship-formation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/friendship.tap` tags: social, friendship, neighbor, water, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil在院子里的水管前排了半个钟头，前面的Mitzi Lechner忽然把桶挪开，让Emil先接。Emil说不用，Mitzi Lechner说你的孩子还在哭。两人就这样认识了；后来Mitzi Lechner替Emil看孩子，Emil替Mitzi Lechner排队买煤，谁也没有说过“帮忙”两个字。
  _effects: flag +friendship_at_the_water_tap; role; skill; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1934.07** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Emil和Elisabeth Kral 2在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Emil母亲上个月的药钱。Elisabeth Kral 2把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Emil把Franz Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Franz Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Emil听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Emil把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Emil在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入)_

- **1935.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Emil当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  Grete Winter怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Emil往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Grete Winter手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Emil一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001629:child_

- **1936.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Emil被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission（其中13克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

  Emil在楼梯间守夜时，邻居说起楼下老太太病重。

  Rosa Winter把Emil带到车站，给了一张去乡下的车票，说案子结之前别回来。Emil只带了一个布包，Otto Weiss站在月台尽头，没有靠近。火车开动时，Emil看见他抬手比了一个割喉的动作，怕他忘了。
  _effects: condition; flag +witness_moved_for_protection; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “the brother knows the train route”_

  Emil在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Maria Hofer 2几眼。散场时Elisabeth Lechner裹着围巾凑过来，Emil低声托Elisabeth Lechner去打听Maria Hofer 2的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Franz Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 29; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Emil把工会开的担保信递进窗口，Rosa Winter看了两遍，又去后面请示，最后在住院单角上写了“缓缴”。Elisabeth Hofer 2把Emil领到靠窗的床位，窗框上还留着上一场雪的水印。

  Emil和Emil Huber在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Emil Huber在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Ferdinand Huber 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Emil排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  Emil把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Emil把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Emil折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1938.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Maria Hofer敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Emil那天刚和Maria Hofer为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Maria Hofer抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Maria Hofer低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Emil还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 2; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007025_

- **1938.07** `r2-law.building-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/stop-work-order` tags: law, building_permit, construction, housing, inspection, vienna, urban, crisis
  脚手架搭到第三层，Rosa Winter带着一张红纸来了，说有人举报超建。Emil说图纸批过，Rosa Winter说批的是两层；Rosa把图纸翻出来，发现角落里的层数被改过，笔迹不是他的。Rosa Winter在脚手架腿上贴了停工令，Anton Bauer在自家窗口看着，窗帘动了一下。
  _effects: cash -12 (脚手架上的批文); condition; flag +construction_halted_for_extra_storey; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; knowledge “the approved drawing shows two storeys, not three”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1938.09** `r3-mig.deportation-appeal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/appeal-dismissed-no-new-facts` tags: migration, deportation, law, appeal, papers, vienna, urban, crisis

- **1919.02** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Anton的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Anton没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Anton发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Rosa Winter 2问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Anton把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Anton把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Josefine Hofer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Anton把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Anton踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Anton单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.01999999999999999 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/folds-notices` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Josefine Hofer带回家。Anton放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Josefine Hofer时，Anton的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。
  _effects: cash 3.2 (folding parish notices); condition; role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Anton拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Gustav Huber弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Anton接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-twice-cleaning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  “想留下，就把教室收拾干净。”Grete Stein把灰桶推到Anton脚边。Anton提着桶下楼梯，灰从桶沿洒出来，在鞋面上落了一层白。擦完课桌，Grete Stein已经坐在炉边，用粉笔头在地上画竖式，让Anton蹲在旁边一道一道算。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1922.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anton想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Alois Lechner 2听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Anton试着站起来，被Alois Lechner 2按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1922.05** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Anton靠在墙边等Josefine Hofer，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Anton开始发烧，Josefine Hofer把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Anton的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Josefine Hofer的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.06** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  “Notwendigkeit”少了一个字母，Berta Winter没说话，只敲了敲桌角。Anton回家时手指上全是墨水，母亲问起，只说写了点作业。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1922.09** `r1-child.library-card::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school::reading-room-culture/confiscated-in-class` tags: library, reading, books, poverty, school, university, culture, schooling
  Anton把书藏在课桌里，被Luise Stein 2没收。Luise Stein 2翻了两页，发现是讲蒸汽机的，放学后把书还回来，说下次别在课上读。Anton问能不能借老师的书，Luise Stein 2说先把这本读完。
  _effects: flag +teacher_lent_book; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; knowledge “the teacher keeps a shelf of engineering books”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Gustav Huber在绳子那头喊。Anton踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Gustav Huber数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Anton把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1923.04** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Anton把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Josef挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Josef转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Anton站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.08** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/pasteboard-boxes-during-closure` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  停课第三天，Josefine Hofer让Anton去印刷所领糊纸盒的活。纸盒堆在床边，Anton糊到半夜，手指被浆糊粘得张不开。Alois Lechner 2来给邻居孩子看病，看见Anton在灯下数纸盒，说这孩子该睡觉。
  _effects: cash 2 (停课十四天); condition; flag +child_labour_during_closure_

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/found-coin-under-rack` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  明信片架子底下有灰，Anton的手指碰到一枚硬东西，摸出来是两赫勒。Alois Lechner 2背对着柜台数报纸，Anton把硬币攥在掌心，指给Alois Lechner 2看一张风景画片。找零时Alois Lechner 2的手停了一下，多看了一眼，还是把画片递了过来。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (买画片花掉捡到的硬币); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.11** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.gift` tags: school, social, childhood
  旧钢笔的笔尖歪向左边，Anton写出来的字母也跟着歪。Josefine Hofer说工具包里翻出来的，凑合用。Grete Stein的红圈画在歪字母旁边。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.apply-for-scholarship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-apply-scholarship-incomplete` tags: task, adolescence, school, scholarship, papers, university, culture, schooling
  Hermann Kral 2把材料推回来，说洗礼证明上的名字和户口册对不上，要先去找教堂更正。Anton跑了三趟，教堂的副本要两克朗。等证明补齐，名额已经发完。Anton学会在每张证明背后写下日期。
  _effects: cash -4 (copies and stamps for the corrected certificate（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.03; flag +scholarship_application_incomplete; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1924.10** `vienna-youth-threshold.school-leaving/stay-home-siblings` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  家里最小的两个还在出疹子，母亲天不亮就得去洗衣房。Anton于是留在家照看弟妹，把离校证明压在镜子后面。街口的杂货铺说等秋天招工再来讲，Anna Winter 2临走时叮嘱别把算术丢下，可水要烧，粥要搅，一天就这么过去了。
  _effects: cash -0.8 (留在家中多烧的煤和额外口粮（其中0.8克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1925.01** `kernel-edu.apply-for-scholarship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-apply-scholarship-awarded` tags: task, adolescence, school, scholarship, papers, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton把成绩单、洗礼证明和Josefine Hofer的收入证明摊在桌上，Berta Winter逐张检查，缺一张就划掉重来。Ferdinand Hofer在窗口盖了三个章，收下两克朗印花税。六周后，通知来了：每年二百四十克朗，先发三十。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 28 (first scholarship payment after the stamp fee); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; flag +scholarship_awarded; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1925.03** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Josefine Hofer病倒在床，Anton留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Anna Winter 2托人带来一包旧课本，Anton在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Anton的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.060000000000000026; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1925.06** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Anton推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Josefine Hofer把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Anton在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Josefine Hofer下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Anton下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1926.01** `task-vienna-poor-apprentice.better-workshop/trial-week-agreed` tags: adolescence, apprentice, work, education, poverty, printshop, task, task:work
  新作坊的工头翻看他的袖口，答应让他试工一周，工钱等试工结束再谈，午饭管一顿。他把旧作坊的活儿做到月底，工头记下他的名字，叫星期一早上七点来。
  _effects: flag +trial_at_new_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.learn-instrument::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/teacher-left` tags: culture, music, instrument, learning, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Stein 2教了三周，忽然说要去外地，把琴谱留给Anton，却没提退学费。Anton抱着琴站在门口，听见楼上的钢琴声一遍遍弹同一段练习曲。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +music_teacher_left; skill_

- **1927.10** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-library` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton在图书馆门口认识Hedwig，两人合用一张借书证，轮流把书带回家。Hedwig读得快，常在书页边留铅笔字；Anton读到那些字，像在跟人说话。冬天过去，书证上多了两个名字。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; skill; flag +study_friend; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/hand-caught` tags: adolescence, labour, union, injury
  Anton在压纸时右手被滚轴卷进去，工头拉下电闸，血滴在铅字盘上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人把Anton送到医院，医药费先由厂里垫付。
  _effects: health -0.11999999999999998 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1928.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Anton就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Anton还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Anton在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1928.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-follow-without-paying` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Anton口袋里没有十赫勒，却还是跟着队伍走到车站。修女在栅栏边拦住Anton，翻了翻名册，说没交钱的孩子不能上车；Maria Hofer 2在车厢门口回头看。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1928.07** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.union-leaflet/union-leaflet.caught` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Anton把传单卷进裤腰，在门口被Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne叫住。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne抽出纸卷念了两行，说“印刷所不是煽动所”，把学徒补贴减半，并要Friedrich Stein 2在排字房当众道歉。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (学徒补贴被减半); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Hermann Huber 2让Anton放学后送两趟货，按趟给钱，天黑前必须回铺子交账。Anton把书包挂在柜台后面，提着篮子穿过三条街；第一趟送错了一户，第二趟跑得鞋底发热。收工时Hermann Huber 2数了三个铜板，说明天还来。
  _effects: cash 2 (放学后的四个钟头); flag +work13_took_part_time_errands; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; employment Laufbursche @ org-die-fackel; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1929.08** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Bauer把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Anton提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Elisabeth Bauer从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated_

- **1930.01** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/held-the-room` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, reform
  首演夜，Anton在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Emil Kral在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Anton的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:100; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.06; skill; flag +debut_success; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/posters-pasted` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien的办事员把一叠防疫告示交给Anton，说浆糊要刷匀，别让风揭了角。Anton沿着市场街贴到天黑，手指被纸边割出细口。第二天，告示下面多了一行铅笔字：“水要煮开”。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

- **1930.08** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  孩子出生后取名Emil Bauer，Elisabeth Bauer一直出血。Anton跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Elisabeth Bauer落下产后虚弱的毛病，Anton抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.2 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine); condition; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:112_

  Anton站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Gustav Huber 2在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Anton摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1931.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Anton手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Rosa Lechner路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1931.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Josef排在前面，回头替Anton看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Anton用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Anton没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004061_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Anton的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:fame` tags: fame, reputation, media, status, state, state_tick, state:fame
  Anton的「公众名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fame state:world-vienna-1912:100_

- **1932.01** `kernel-work.task-household-debt::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/work-debt-reduced` tags: task, work, debt, household, credit, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anton把欠账按日期抄在纸上，先还利息最高的一笔。Alois Bauer翻开米铺账本，把红字划掉一行，写“收到八克朗”。为了凑这笔钱，Anton把家里一只旧表送进当铺；Friedrich Weiss知道后，把自家的赊账往后挪了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (偿还米铺欠账（其中8克朗记入债务）); role; flag +debt_reduced; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; knowledge “米铺把赊账利息记在红字栏，先还红字最多的那笔最划算。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1932.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.05 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Anton和Elisabeth Bauer把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.08000000000000002; mood -0.07999999999999996; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.pay-deposit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Anton从内袋里数出两克朗压在柜台上，Rosa Winter 2翻开借书卡，用铅笔把上次迟还的四天圈出来，还是把小说递了过来。Anton把书塞进外套里侧，出门就翻到第一页，边走边读，差点撞上推手推车的菜贩；两克朗换来的不只是一本小说，还有一整个下午的安静。
  _effects: cash -2 (租书店借小说的押金); skill; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1933.04** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Anton把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Gustav Berg 2放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Maria Hofer 2在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1933.06** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-look-only` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Anton在人群外站到腿发酸，只隔着跳舞的人看了Maria Hofer 3几眼。散场时Alois Bauer裹着围巾凑过来，Anton低声托Alois Bauer去打听Maria Hofer 3的门第和姓氏，自己先出了酒馆，在冷风里等回话；一整晚没敢上前，鞋底粘着酒馆地板的糖渍。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Gustav Berg 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Gustav Berg 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1934.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.no-vacancy` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  Rosa Stein 3没有接话，先从抽屉里撕下一角纸。Anton把名字、住址和手艺一样样说完，工头边写边应，写完把纸压回抽屉，说月底来问。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1934.05** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Friedrich Winter在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Winter在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; mood 0.04000000000000001; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1934.07** `r13-rel.introduction-friend::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::introduction-friend-rural/apples-at-the-stall` tags: relationship, introduction, friend, acquaintance, social, rural, agriculture, small_town
  集市散了一半，Elisabeth Winter才把Otto Winter 2领到Anton的摊前，说两家住得不远。Otto Winter 2捏了捏Anton摊上的苹果，问是不是自家园子的。旁边卖布的女人一直看着，Elisabeth Winter把声音压得更低。
  _effects: role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000385:child_

- **1934.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money, injury
  Anton和Hedwig把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Hedwig守前半夜，Anton守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Anton的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josefine Hofer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: health -0.05 (连续夜里守夜睡眠不足); relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +night_care_duty; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1934.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004196_

  Anton把Friedrich Winter领进厨房，Josefine Hofer正在切面包，刀没有停。Friedrich Winter把带来的果酱放在桌角，说自家做的；Josefine Hofer看了一眼，让它搁着。吃饭时，Josefine Hofer问了三遍家里的情况，第三遍时语气松了；Friedrich Winter走时，Josefine Hofer把剩下的半条面包塞进了Friedrich Winter的口袋。
  _effects: cash -2 (星期天的第三副碗筷); flag +partner_accepted_at_family_table; role; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the mother sent half a loaf home with the partner”_

  _effects: mood -0.14; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:158_

  Maria Hofer把两张床之间的布帘拉开，说“第三个人睡地板，早上卷起来”。Friedrich Moser把自己的大衣铺在草垫上，算是让出一半。Anton数了数房钱，发现自己付的是床位的价，不是地板的价。夜里有人翻身，三个人都醒着。
  _effects: cash -4 (宿舍里的第三张床); condition; flag +shared_dormitory_floor; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  Josefine Hofer在Anton的门框上画了一个白叉，说这一层要“清理”，外国人的房租从下月起加两克朗。Anton问租约上没写这一条，Josefine Hofer说租约是旧政府的，现在归他解释。Hermann Weiss在隔壁把门开了一条缝，又轻轻关上。
  _effects: cash -4 (门上的粉笔叉); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +marked_with_chalk_cross; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the landlord marks foreign tenants with a chalk cross”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; condition; flag +slept_on_kitchen_bench_after_return; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “the partner kept a bowl of soup warm”_

- **1938.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton把通知念给Alois Bauer 2听，Alois Bauer 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Anton去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1938.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Rosa Winter 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Rosa Winter 2陪着Anton去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1938.08** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Anton把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Anton睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Josef Moser在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Josef Moser在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001211:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1939.01** `state_expire:grief` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick, state:grief

  _effects: state_remove grief state:world-vienna-1912:158_

- **1939.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/church-concert` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  Anton算了一下，四克朗够一个星期午饭。他改约Elisabeth Winter去教堂听免费的管风琴音乐会，捐了两赫勒，坐在后排的长凳上。回去时两人在街角分吃一个烤土豆。
  _effects: cash -0.06 (教堂捐献与烤土豆); food; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000385:child_

- **1939.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Rosa Moser 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1939.11** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  屋里只有一张床，Anton把椅子拼在床边守了三夜，给Josefine Hofer敷药换布。请假扣的工钱让人心疼，但腿伤见好；第四天早上，Josefine Hofer自己端起了粥碗。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1939.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Rosa Stein排在前面，回头替Anton看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Anton用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001314:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton看Rosa Moser 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Rosa Moser 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Anton门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Anton打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007622_

  Emil Kral在通行证上盖了章，又用铅笔在角落写了一个日期。Anton拿到许可，可以去城西的修配厂上工；走出劳动局时，Anton把证件摸了三遍，确认它还在口袋里。
  _effects: cash -2 (通行证手续费); role; flag +work_permit_granted; knowledge “城西修配厂只认盖蓝章的通行证。”_

  话说到一半，Luise Bauer又把三年前的旧账翻出来，声音越来越高。Anton把杯子放下，没有还嘴，只把桌上的水渍擦干。Luise Bauer忽然停下来，问Anton是不是觉得自己有理；Anton说不是，只是不想再摔第二个杯子。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +friendship_truce; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  槌子落在第七号，Anton的院子归了一个外地来的粮商。Ferdinand Huber 2让Anton在十五天内搬走，Luise Stein 2把清单念到“灶台一座”时停了一下。Hermann Huber把自家的板车推来，说先装要紧的，家具以后再说。
  _effects: cash -10 (拍卖槌落在第七号); condition; flag +home_sold_at_auction; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the auction buyer is a grain merchant from outside the distr”_

- **1940.06** `r1-work.street-vendor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/paid-the-hawker-fine` tags: commerce, street, vendor, police, permit, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Weiss用警棍敲了敲推车轮子，说没有牌照不能占人行道。Anton把卖剩的萝卜往车里拢，Otto Weiss还是撕下三克朗的罚单，蓝纸在风里抖。Hermann Huber攥着零钱等在旁边，Anton把铜板数了两遍，最后少找了十赫勒，谁也没有说破。
  _effects: cash -3 (巡警来了，推车还在坡上); flag +paid_hawker_fine; role; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child_

  房东发现多住了一个人，扬言要涨租。Anton和Friedrich Stein 2连夜把床单拆下来，把Friedrich Stein 2的箱子藏进壁橱。第二天房东来查，只看见桌上多了一副碗筷。
  _effects: cash -2 (一张床与两条被子); flag +hid_lodger_from_landlord; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; knowledge “the landlord does not know about the second lodger”_

  Anton把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Anton知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  Anton把面粉撒在桌上抹平，用指头写了一个“M”，让Josefine Hofer照着画。Josefine Hofer的手指粗得像胡萝卜，第一笔就戳穿了面层；Anton又抹平一次，这次握着老人的手腕慢慢走。写到自己的名字时，Josefine Hofer忽然笑了，说原来这几个弯是这个意思。
  _effects: flag +taught_parent_to_write_name; role; skill; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

- **1941.03** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Anton挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Maria Hofer敲门时，Anton从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1941.07** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/hungry-winter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  Anton用配给卡换到半袋面粉和几根冻土豆。锅里煮的是稀汤，邻居送来一小块人造黄油，说孩子们先吃。
  _effects: food; condition_

- **1941.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Anton把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Otto Weiss把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Rosa Winter不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Anton扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.14000000000000012; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”_

- **1942.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Ferdinand Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Ferdinand Bauer敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Anton把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.07** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/landlord-called-police` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton拒绝加租，Karl Kraus第二天带着警察来，说屋里有六个人登记，实际住了九个。Maria Hofer站在楼梯口，说愿意替Anton作证只住了四个。警察数了数床垫，把数字写在本子上，房东在旁边补充“他们晚上还烧煤油炉”。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +landlord_reported_overcrowding; relation rival with person-karl-kraus; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; knowledge “the landlord counts mattresses for the police”_

- **1942.07** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Katharina Hofer把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Katharina Hofer在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1943.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/rain-ruined-two-boxes` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, crisis
  半路下起雨，Anton把油布往箱子上盖，风却把一角掀开。送到时，最上面两只纸箱已经软了，里面的货染了水汽。店主把湿箱挑出来，从货款里扣了五克朗。Ferdinand Weiss说这条街的排水沟一堵，雨就专往板车上灌。
  _effects: cash -5 (板车上的十二箱); condition; flag +work13_delivery_ruined_by_rain; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111_

- **1943.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Josef Moser把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Anton说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Otto Winter 2在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Anton的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001211:child; reputation -0.07_

- **1943.06** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Anton对Gustav Kral说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Gustav Kral把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Anton的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Anton把纸条卷进自行车内胎，打足气，骑过检查站时还按了两下车铃。Ferdinand Huber 2伸手拦车，问后座绑的什么；Anton说是给修女送的面粉。Ferdinand Huber 2捏了捏轮胎，手指沾了一点黑油，挥手放行。骑出半里，Anton才发现后背全湿了。
  _effects: flag +carried_message_in_tire; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the checkpoint does not inspect tire pressure”_

- **1943.08** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/testify` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Anton在警察局走廊里等了一下午，最后把知道的地址写在纸上。回家时，楼道里有人把门关得很轻；Anton知道他们听见了脚步声。
  _effects: organization_membership; reputation -0.05_

  Rosa Moser在第一个人面前就站住了，说那件灰外套不会错。Alois Bauer没有再看Anton的眼睛，只让警士把手铐拿出来。Anton被推上囚车时，听见Rosa Moser在身后说：“我记得那个人走路的样子。”其实Anton的左脚并不跛。
  _effects: state_add detained state:world-vienna-1912:278; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001012:child; flag +identified_by_witness; flag +detained_

  警报响时Anton躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.donate-clothes::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/coat-returned` tags: social, charity, clothing, poverty, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  第二天，Rosa Winter把外套送回来，说领口里缝着一张名字条，怕原主人家还要。Anton这才想起父亲总在衣服里侧写名字。Rosa Winter把外套搭在椅背上，说改一改还能给孩子穿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +donated_coat_returned; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Berta Stein从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Anton把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Anton把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001113:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  汽车没有停，轮胎在湿石板上打了个滑。Anton记住了车牌的前两位，Friedrich Moser 2说那辆车常在市政厅后门停。第二天警察来问话，记完就走了；Anton的胯骨疼了三个月。

  炮火把Anton住的那条街烧掉一半。Anton跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter)_

  Anton的「被拘留」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove detained state:world-vienna-1912:278; flag -detained; flag +released_from_detention_

  Anton把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Anton折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1945.02** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1945.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1946.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Anton把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Anton知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fed-neighbor` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton隔着门给Ferdinand Stein递了一锅汤，Ferdinand Stein在门里咳得说不成句。Anton把锅放在门槛上，退回白线外，看那只手把锅端进去。第二天，白线外又多了一道线。
  _effects: cash -1 (门框上的石灰线); food; flag +fed_quarantined_neighbor_

- **1947.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/backyard-stove` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  Otto Weiss 的话很简短：名单不能批。Anton 把表格折好，转头去搬砖搭灶。Rosa Moser 2 从家里抱来一捆柴，火星溅在围裙上，拍两下就灭了。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Anton攥紧扶手。Anton Stein站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/leave-unsigned` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton把文件推回Alois Bauer面前，说不签，等Josefine Hofer自己改主意。Josefine Hofer的嘴角动了动，Hedwig把三把钥匙一把把收进衣袋，金属碰着金属响了几声。Anton走下公证处的石阶，街对面面包房正在出炉，热气扑到脸上，口袋里一个赫勒也没多，房契的事仍旧悬着。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +inheritance_unsigned_

- **1947.09** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/moves-the-bed-in` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Josefine Hofer在楼梯口喘了两次才上到三楼。Anton没有开会，也没有和谁商量，晚上就把折叠床搬进走廊尽头的小房间，把闹钟拨到凌晨两点。第二天，Anton在药盒上贴了第一张写着时间的纸。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:325; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +moved_parent_into_the_flat_

- **1947.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Anton嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Anton用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; reputation 0.04_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Maria Stein抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Maria Stein用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000549:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011359_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Anton一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Alois Lechner送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1948.01** `lc17-care.crisis-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-laugh` tags: adulthood, care, eldercare, night, family, exhaustion, illness, vienna
  Josefine Hofer把Anton认成了已经去世的配偶，问今天怎么没有买李子。Anton先纠正，后来顺着说“卖完了”，Josefine Hofer便发起脾气。Anton走到厨房，靠着水池笑了一声，又赶紧捂住嘴；那笑声在瓷砖上弹回来，听着像哭。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 30 -> 38; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 10 -> 20; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +parent_mistook_me; knowledge “老人把我认成了已经去世的配偶，问今天为什么没有买李子。”_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  {patient}今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Anton在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 46; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 20 -> 26; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees)_

- **1948.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Lechner把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Anton去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Grete Huber 2给Anton一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Anton把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1948.02** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home
  Josefine Hofer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Anton晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱)_

- **1948.10** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/language-test-failed` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Stein让Anton用官方语言读一段公民义务，Anton读到“纳税”时卡住了。Gustav Stein说可以补考，但要交两克朗，还要一张学校证明。Anna Winter愿意写证明，可学校说Anton只上过夜校，没有正式学籍；Gustav Stein把材料推回来，说下次再来。
  _effects: cash -2 (入籍宣誓前的口试); condition; flag +failed_citizenship_language_test; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000258:child; knowledge “the language test requires a formal school certificate”_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  {patient}今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Anton在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 46 -> 54; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 26 -> 32; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees)_

- **1949.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.half` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  办事处的职员把信读了第二遍，在清单上添了Anton的名字。存款八十四克朗，一人四十二。兄弟签完字把笔按在纸上，出了门就往另一头走。Anton把怀表留在桌上，说这个给你，兄弟没回头。
  _effects: cash 42 (分得父亲存款的一半); relation_end kin with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Friedrich Moser把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Anton说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Hedwig在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Anton的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; reputation -0.07_

- **1949.07** `r2-law.fire-insurance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rider-paid` tags: finance, insurance, fire, contract, housing, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton在火灾前加保了明火险，保费比原来贵一倍。Hermann Huber收了钱，在保单上贴了一张附加条；Ferdinand Huber 2来查时，Anton把炉子指给他看，说烟囱是新砌的。赔付下来时，Anton先还了Ferdinand Hofer的借款。
  _effects: cash 16 (保单背面的小字); flag +fire_claim_paid_with_rider; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “the rider covers open-flame heating if the chimney is inspec”_

- **1949.09** `r1-civ.requisition-cart::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cart-and-horse-requisitioned` tags: war, requisition, property, army, shortage, vienna, urban, postwar
  军队征收站设在市场边上，Alois Bauer把一张征收单拍在Anton的车上，说要征用马和车，按市价给收据。Anton问收据什么时候能兑，Alois Bauer说“战后”。Karl Kraus在旁边牵着自己的驴，缰绳攥得发白。
  _effects: cash -8 (征收单上的马); condition; flag +cart_and_horse_requisitioned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation colleague with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “the receipt is redeemable after the war, without interest”_

- **1949.10** `r3-mig.passport-queue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-passport-exchange/window-closed-at-noon` tags: migration, papers, queue, bureaucracy, work, vienna, urban, postwar
  天没亮Anton就到市政厅门口排队，前面已经有八十多人。铁门十一点才开，Mitzi Lechner出来挂了一块牌子：今日号已发完。队伍没有散，雨把号码纸泡烂了。Mitzi Lechner说下周四再来，Anton算了一下，那天正好轮到自己上工。
  _effects: cash -1 (雨里的护照队); condition; flag +missed_passport_window; knowledge “passport numbers are exhausted before noon”_

- **1950.01** `lc17-care.last-letter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/dies-in-hospital` tags: adulthood, care, death, inheritance, grief, family, funeral, vienna
  医院在第七层，电梯坏了两天。Josefine Hofer最后说的话是“把窗开一点”，Anton去开窗时，护士已经在拉帘子。葬礼从简，棺材是租的；Alois Bauer把遗嘱念到第二页，提到一只旧怀表，表壳里还夹着一张一九一四年的车票。回家后，Anton的膝盖开始积水，医生说是长期搬抬和睡硬椅留下的。
  _effects: death (长期病弱); state_remove lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:325; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:375; state_add chronic-illness state:world-vienna-1912:376; cash -3 (hospital fees and a rented coffin); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +inherited_watch; knowledge “怀表里夹着一张一九一四年的车票。”_

- **1950.01** `r13-work.advertise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/poster-brought-customers` tags: commerce, advertising, shop, competition, literacy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Moser用红色油墨印了三十张海报，Anton把它们贴在面包房、澡堂和电车站的柱子上。海报上写着“新到咖啡，一磅九赫勒”，字歪了一点，价格却看得清。第三天，三个从没进过店的客人拿着海报来问，Anton把海报上的价又让了半赫勒。
  _effects: cash 5 (橱窗里的手写海报); reputation 0.05; flag +work13_poster_brought_customers; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1950.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Anton听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Mitzi Bauer 3扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1950.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa Moser 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa Moser 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Anton把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1951.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013133_

- **1951.01** `fm17-consolation.kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-argument-about-heaven` tags: faith, consolation, grief, family, neighbor, care, quiet, vienna
  Rosa Bauer说人死了会在天上重逢，Anton说没有证据，两个人从厨房吵到楼梯口。吵到最后，Rosa Bauer承认自己也不知道，只是愿意这么想；Anton靠在栏杆上笑了一下，说“那你刚才还说得那么肯定”。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Anton在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

  Anton的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: health -0.05 (chronic illness worsened); cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.45 -> 0.55_

  Anton陪Anna Kral 3去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Anna Kral 3用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Anton没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

  Anton提议先派两个人去见厂主，谈不成再投票。Alois Bauer嫌太软，Friedrich Moser却点头，说家里等着月底的工钱。最后表决，派代表的动议多出四票，Anton被记在代表名单的第二行。
  _effects: skill; flag +union_delegate_named_

- **1951.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Berta Weiss 3，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Berta Weiss 3点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

  Anton又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anton的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。

- **1952.01** `r14-fam.sunday-rota::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rota-signed` tags: task, family, divorce, co_parenting, custody, vienna, urban, postwar
  两人在厨房桌上把星期几、几点、谁去接孩子一条条写下来。Josef Moser写到星期三时停住，说那天要上晚班；Anton把星期三换到星期四，又在旁边画了一个圈。Elisabeth Bauer在门口听着，最后把那张纸拿过去，用彩笔在周末那格画了一只小船。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +co_parenting_rota; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001211:child; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:011049:child_

- **1952.02** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Anna Kral 3把Anton寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Anton追到码头时，Anna Kral 3已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Anton在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.05** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Friedrich Moser 2排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Anton，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1952.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Ferdinand Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Ferdinand Weiss敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-111; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1953.01** `fm17-relic.healing-line::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-change-at-all` tags: faith, relic, healing, medicine, chronic_illness, pilgrimage, doubt, vienna
  轮到Anton时，守堂人已经把玻璃匣擦了三遍。Anton按了很久，膝盖还是原来的膝盖；Friedrich Moser说这是信心还不够，又说可以先买一小瓶圣水。Anton没有买，只在长椅上坐到队伍散尽，听扫地的声音从祭坛那边过来。
  _effects: cash -2 (进城的车钱与蜡烛); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.65 -> 0.67; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +fm17_relic_no_change_

- **1953.01** `state_expire:grief` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick, state:grief
  Anton的「丧亲之痛」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove grief state:world-vienna-1912:375_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick
  Anton的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.67 -> 0.77_

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sheltered-two-nights` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Lechner站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Anton把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Mitzi Lechner在梦里说了一种他听不懂的话，Anton把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights_

- **1953.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton把Anton Stein和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Anton把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000687:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1953.09** `r3-mig.train-compartment::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/same-district-stranger` tags: migration, train, travel, stranger, borders, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton在包厢里啃干面包，Alois Lechner 2问是不是从河东来的。三句话之后，两个人认出了同一个集市。Alois Lechner 2把地址写在烟盒纸上，说到了那边可以找他舅舅。Friedrich Moser进来查票，看见两个人挤在一个座位上，什么也没说。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +met_stranger_from_same_district; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; knowledge “the stranger’s uncle lives near the destination”_

- **1953.12** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Anton在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Katharina Hofer的门口。傍晚Katharina Hofer来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1954.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Josef Moser 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Josef Moser 2在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/stable` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick
  Anton又撑过了一年。病没有走，但也没有立刻把人带走。
  _effects: state_parameter chronic-illness.years 0 -> 1_

- **1954.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  Anton把半天工的工钱单交回去，换回自己看孩子。晚饭从两顿并成一顿，土豆和面包皮煮一锅，伴侣把煤钱数了两遍。孩子睡下后，Anton在窗边缝袜子，一枚针扎了手指，她含住指头继续缝。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入); food; health -0.04 (营养不足和夜里缝补的劳累)_

- **1954.02** `r13-turn.debt-crisis::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-rent-arrears/defaulted` tags: finance, debt, poverty, household, pressure, vienna, urban, postwar
  第七天，Friedrich Moser带着两个人在门口等到天黑。Anton把家里的现金全数出来，还差九克朗。Friedrich Moser没有砸门，只把一张法院传票从门缝塞进来，说下次来的是法警。Maria Huber在对面把门关得很轻。
  _effects: cash -20 (催款单贴在门框上); condition; flag +debt_default; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; knowledge “a court summons follows the unpaid debt”_

- **1954.08** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Josef Moser在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001211:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Hermann Berg攥着Anton的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1955.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Anton的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Anton看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015639_

  名单墙前挤着十几个人，Gustav Huber 2指着一行被划掉的名字，说那是他妹妹。他替对方抄下名字，又去Ferdinand Huber 2的窗口问；窗口说档案在另一栋楼，他去时那栋楼已经锁门。他在档案楼门口等到锁门，只记下了一个门牌号。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; knowledge “名单上被划掉的名字要查另一栋楼的档案，窗口关门后只能等明天”; cash -3 (抄名单的纸笔和跑两栋楼的车钱); flag +searched_refugee_list_

- **1956.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.postcard` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  问询表要出生日期和最后通信地址，Anton写到一半停了笔，把哥哥的信从证件夹里翻出来照着抄。寄出去后每天看门房有没有信。两个月后明信片送到，画面是鲁尔河，背面铅笔字被雨水洇过。
  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”_

- **1956.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Franz Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Franz Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005377:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  轮到Anton时，守堂人已经把玻璃匣擦了三遍。Anton按了很久，膝盖还是原来的膝盖；Otto Bauer 2说这是信心还不够，又说可以先买一小瓶圣水。Anton没有买，只在长椅上坐到队伍散尽，听扫地的声音从祭坛那边过来。
  _effects: cash -2 (进城的车钱与蜡烛); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 0.87 -> 0.89; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  阁楼的窗户用黑布蒙住，桌上摊着一份手抄名单，每个人的名字后面画着一颗星。Anna Winter教Anton敲门：三下慢，两下快。楼下忽然有人用警棍敲水管，所有人同时把手按在名单上。Friedrich Moser 2低声说，后楼梯的雪已经扫过了。
  _effects: condition; flag +joined_secret_society; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000258:child; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; organization_membership; knowledge “the attic knock is three slow and two quick”_

- **1957.01** `r14-soc.gamble-away-wages::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lost-the-week` tags: task, gambling_debt, gambling, money, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  赛马场外的泥地上，Maria Hofer 2把Anton的工钱数进帽子里，说“下周翻本”。Grete Huber拽了拽Anton的袖子，Anton没有动。回家的电车上，Anton把口袋翻了三遍，只摸到一张赛马票的存根。第二天上工，Anton把工票藏在鞋垫下面，怕家里翻出来。那天夜里，Anton把赛马票的存根烧了，灰留在鞋垫上。
  _effects: cash -12 (第三个栏架前的马); mood -0.25; condition; flag +gambling_debt; knowledge “the bookmaker takes bets behind the tram depot”_

  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Anton蹲在石阶上替Helene 2守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007680:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1958.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Anton在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Anton拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Otto Weiss找到Anton时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1958.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Anton蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.01000000000000012_

- **1958.03** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  Anton把腌鲱鱼放在黑面包上，咸味够配一大杯水。Franz Winter 2拿着盐罐过来，看见鱼就说拿卷心菜换。换完以后，两家灶上都多了一样东西。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1958.08** `r1-fam.friendship-formation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/friendship.tap` tags: social, friendship, neighbor, water, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton在院子里的水管前排了半个钟头，前面的Elisabeth Berg忽然把桶挪开，让Anton先接。Anton说不用，Elisabeth Berg说你的孩子还在哭。两人就这样认识了；后来Elisabeth Berg替Anton看孩子，Anton替Elisabeth Berg排队买煤，谁也没有说过“帮忙”两个字。
  _effects: flag +friendship_at_the_water_tap; role; skill_

  Franz Winter把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Anton去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Anton Kral给Anton一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Anton把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1959.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton先给Hedwig盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Hedwig吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1959.08** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Maria Weiss却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Anton把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1959.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Anton去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Rosa Huber 2路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1960.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick
  Anton的病在今年加重。医生换了一种药，账单比药更让人喘不过气。
  _effects: cash -2 (medical costs); state_parameter chronic-illness.severity 1 -> 1_

- **1960.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/withdrew-to-keep-the-club` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton在投票前站起来，说今年不选了，让Rosa Huber 3连任，条件是账目每月贴在门口。Rosa Huber 3答应得很快。散会后，几个老团员拍Anton的肩，说“你倒像当家的”。第二个月，账目真的贴在了门口，第一页的墨迹被雨淋得发花。第一页是空白的，第二页才开始有字。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; reputation 0.04; flag +withdrew_from_club_election; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-179; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-179_

- **1960.05** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Anton和Friedrich Moser 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Anton家，钥匙只在白天给。Friedrich Moser 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Berta Weiss 3听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Anton把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1960.07** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-finished-paid` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor
  书箱比看起来沉，Anton一个人抱下楼时膝盖直打颤。Alois Kral把午饭摆在新住处的窗台上，黑面包夹着咸肉，汤用旧罐子装着。工钱给的是铜币，数起来哗啦响。
  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱)_

- **1961.01** `fm17-relic.healing-line::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-line-is-too-long` tags: faith, relic, healing, medicine, chronic_illness, pilgrimage, doubt, vienna
  队伍从侧廊一直排到教堂门口。一个抱着孩子的女人被守堂人领到前面，说孩子病得急；Anton往后退了一步，再往后退一步，最后退到了街上。Mitzi Stein追出来说下周四还有一次，Anton点点头，没有问下周四自己还走不走得动。

- **1961.01** `state_tick:chronic-illness/body-year/worse` tags: chronic-illness, health, illness, body, state, state_tick

- **1961.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/recognized-neighbor` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton舀到第七碗时，认出碗后面那张脸是楼下的Hermann Huber。Hermann Huber把帽檐压低，Anton什么也没问，只把勺子沉下去，多捞了一块土豆。后面的人开始敲碗。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +saw_neighbor_in_soup_line; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the neighbor has been queueing at the soup kitchen”_

  介绍所的门关到一半，里面还在喊下一位。Anton和Grete Huber站在廊下，听着货栈、面粉厂、马厩各处的消息，最后只记住北站那条。烟盒纸塞进上衣口袋，走路时窸窸窣窣响。
  _effects: flag +has_work_lead_

- **1961.09** `r2-faith.foundling-wheel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sent-to-the-foundling-home` tags: charity, foundling, infant, church, secrecy, vienna, urban, postwar
  清晨开门时，转门里有个藤篮，孩子身上别着一张纸条，只写了“若活着，请取名若瑟”。Anton把篮子提进圣器室，Grete Huber来登记时问母亲姓名，Anton说纸条上没有。孩子被送去育婴堂，篮子留在门房装煤。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +foundling_registered_without_mother_name; role; knowledge “the note asked that the child be named Joseph”_

- **1961.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Ferdinand Lechner 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1962.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Lechner 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Lechner 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。

- **1916.01** `statistical_infection`

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/enrolled` tags: childhood, school, fees
  Josefine Hofer带Hedwig到Leopoldstädter Volksschule报名。Grete Stein在名册上写下孩子的姓名和出生月份，收了两克朗书本费，让孩子第二天带一块石板来。
  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books); role; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-list-instead` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  锁门的人在走廊里咳嗽了一声。Berta Winter一边道歉一边收拾，最后从粉笔盒底下抽出一张旧练习纸，圈了四道题。Hedwig接过来，想问第二题怎么开头，Berta Winter已经提着包下楼了。校门口还有太阳，Hedwig把纸摊在台阶上看了一会儿。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/punished` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  放学路上，Hedwig把手揣在口袋里，碰一下就疼。回到家门口，Hedwig先把手背在身后，等Josefine Hofer转身才敢看；那道红印到晚上还没有消。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (punishment at school); condition; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Rosa Winter 2从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Hedwig。Hedwig把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1920.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/truancy-notice` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig在巷口替人跑腿，忘了下午的课。第二天督学站在教室后排，Berta Winter把缺课的名字念出来，Josefine Hofer被叫到学校，罚了两克朗，回家路上没有跟Hedwig说话。那晚Hedwig把跑腿挣的两枚硬币放在桌上，谁也没动。第二天一早自己去了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (点名册上的第三个空位); mood -0.05000000000000002; flag +truancy_notice; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.05** `vienna-school-childhood.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.extra` tags: school, social, childhood
  Hedwig放学后留在学校汤厨房帮Ferdinand Stein搅锅，木勺比Hedwig的手臂还长。Ferdinand Stein多给了Hedwig一勺扁豆汤，Hedwig用铁皮饭盒带回家，倒进母亲的锅里。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1920.07** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/temperature-line-in-soot` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  黑板旁贴了张告示：猩红热，停课两周。Ferdinand Stein让每个孩子带一张纸回家，上面抄着“每日体温”。Hedwig不会写“体温”，Josefine Hofer用灶灰在桌上画了一道线，说高过这条就说话。
  _effects: condition; flag +school_closed_for_scarlet_fever; skill; education primary_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Hedwig放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Hedwig的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Hedwig也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Hedwig用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Josefine Hofer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Hedwig的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_
