  Josefine Hofer在饭桌上说，既然考不上，就去印刷所当学徒，至少能挣口饭。Hedwig第二天跟着Josefine Hofer去见工头，围裙比身子大一圈。机器响起来时，Hedwig把师傅说的铅字字号记在手心，晚上洗手时全花了。第一个月工钱两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (放榜墙上的空白); flag +apprenticed_after_failure; role; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/silent-follow` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  推车的轮子碾过石板，Hedwig隔着两步跟在后面，一直跟到装订铺后门。Rosa Winter 2卸纸时看了Hedwig一眼，问“找活？”，Hedwig说是，伸手去接最上面一捆。三捆纸搬完，手腕外侧已经肿起一条筋；Rosa Winter 2说师傅今天不在，让改天再来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +seeking_work_

- **1925.04** `r3-teen.dormitory-theft::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/blamed` tags: education, dormitory, theft, trust, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Winter 2在Hedwig的洗衣袋里找到了Maria Hofer 2丢的表，表链上还缠着一根红线。全宿舍站成一排，Maria Hofer 2没有看Hedwig。她说不是自己拿的，声音在瓷砖墙上撞回来，显得很轻。
  _effects: reputation -0.1; condition; flag +accused_of_dormitory_theft; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1925.11** `r1-child.gymnasium-books::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university::university-reading-room/ring-for-tuition` tags: gifted, gymnasium, books, class, teacher, university, culture, schooling
  Ferdinand Stein在成绩单上写“建议升入文法中学”，又用红笔圈了学费。Josefine Hofer把戒指摘下来，放在当铺柜台上，换回十二克朗。Hedwig背着新书包去考试，书包里只有一本借来的拉丁语课本。
  _effects: cash -12 (拉丁文的旧课本（其中12克朗记入债务）); flag +family_sold_ring_for_school; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; education secondary_

- **1926.01** `kernel-edu.ask-for-recommendation::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::edu-recommend-reform/edu-recommend-reform-lost` tags: task, adolescence, school, teacher, recommendation, reform, medical, schooling
  表格在传递中丢了。Hedwig跑了两趟指导处，第三次才拿到副本，截止日期已经过了。Luise Stein 2替Hedwig写了一封说明信，能不能补上，要等下周。那一周Hedwig每天放学都去指导处问一次。
  _effects: cash -1 (copies and tram fare（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); flag +recommendation_form_lost; reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Rosa Winter 2在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Hedwig下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill_

- **1926.09** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, school, teacher
  Hedwig的成绩打动了本地学校，拿到一份覆盖学费的奖学金；家里第一次不用为这笔钱吵到半夜。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:65_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Hedwig分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Hedwig的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:scholarship-holder/annual-renewal/revoked` tags: scholarship-holder, education, scholarship, means_tested, state, state_tick, night-school
  Hedwig的奖学金被取消，学校把过去一年的费用开成一张账单。
  _effects: state_transition scholarship-holder -> night-school; cash -60 (back-dated tuition bill（其中58.4克朗记入债务）)_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.library-study::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/inventory-closed` tags: education, library, study, reading, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig赶到时，阅览室门口挂着“盘点停开”的牌子。Hedwig在台阶上坐到天黑，把带来的笔记背了两遍，回家时鞋底湿透，第二天发起低烧。低烧退了以后，Hedwig再去，门口换了新牌子：阅览室每天只开三个钟头，Hedwig把借书证收进抽屉，等秋天再来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1927.06** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Moser看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Josefine Hofer求了半天，Ferdinand Moser答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Anton干了三天就走了，Hedwig留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Ferdinand Moser才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1927.07** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Hedwig在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Elisabeth Bauer 2跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Hedwig去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.11** `r1-child.cuff-answers::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-examination-board/oral-examination` tags: exam, cheating, school, shame, numeracy, reform, medical, schooling
  考试委员会改用口试，Rosa Winter 2坐在长桌后面，面前摆着一排编号的木牌。Hedwig抽到第七号，题目是“说出三个分数的加法”。她站在屋子中央，听见自己的声音在墙上弹回来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +oral_exam_introduced; skill_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/hand-caught` tags: adolescence, labour, union, injury
  Hedwig在压纸时右手被滚轴卷进去，工头拉下电闸，血滴在铅字盘上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人把Hedwig送到医院，医药费先由厂里垫付。
  _effects: health -0.11999999999999998 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport)_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/held` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又撑过一年夜校；工位和课桌仍然互相偷时间，但进度在缓慢累积。
  _effects: state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 15_

- **1928.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Berg把一条绷带绕在Hedwig的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Hedwig在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Mitzi Berg发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1928.02** `r3-teen.dropout-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/sent-to-work` tags: education, dropout, return, poverty, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer在饭桌上说，学校坐不起了，明天去印刷所找活。Hedwig把没写完的作业本塞进灶膛，火苗舔到最后一页。Berta Winter来家里问过一次，家里只说孩子病了。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +left_school_for_printshop_work; role; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; education none_

- **1928.04** `r3-teen.reform-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-points-system/escape` tags: reform_school, confinement, craft, escape, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig趁放风时翻过围墙，沿着铁路走了半夜。天亮前，他在编组站被巡夜人发现，送回所里时，鞋丢了一只。所长让他在禁闭室待了两天，墙上只有一道透光的缝。回到工场后，Ferdinand Stein没有问他为什么跑，只把一双旧鞋放在床脚。
  _effects: mood -0.11999999999999994; condition; flag +escaped_reform_school; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1928.08** `r3-teen.military-cadet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/injured` tags: military, cadet, exam, injury, uniform, reform, medical, schooling
  跨越木马时，Hedwig的左手撑偏了，落地时手腕折出一个不该有的角度。Josefine Hofer让人把他抬到医务室，军医说骨头接上也不能再握枪。Hedwig看着自己的手，第一次觉得它这么陌生。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +cadet_injured_in_drill; unemployment (discharged after a training injury)_

- **1928.08** `state_reaction:night-school/reaction-unemployment` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_reaction, military, cadet
  Hedwig失业后第一次认真考虑把夜校读到底。
  _effects: flag +night-school:reacted_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003155_

- **1929.01** `task-vienna-youth-ask-old-worker/too-busy` tags: task, task:education, work, apprenticeship, learning
  Hedwig刚开口，老工人就把字盘推回架子上，说今天还有一版急活。Hedwig只好把问题咽回去，站在旁边看了半刻钟，学会的只有怎么把废纸折成垫片。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “老工人忙的时候，问手艺要等收工以后。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  Hedwig用Lena借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Luise Stein 2把Hedwig的名字念了两遍，Lena站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; education secondary_

  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:101; reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +denounced_by_known_person; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  Hedwig的试讲被督学打断，督学说新方法太散，还是背书写字牢靠。Hedwig没有争辩，在课后把两种教案都写了一遍，附上学生的作业对比。督学看完，没有表态，只把材料带走了，Hedwig在门口站到天黑。教案被留在督学的公文包里。
  _effects: flag +pedagogy_dispute; skill_

  Elisabeth Bauer 2把钱推过来，Hedwig又推回去。Hedwig说自己只是倒霉，不是什么会看事的人。Elisabeth Bauer 2把钱留在桌上走了，第二天又派人来取。Hedwig把那一夜省下来的蜡烛收进柜子，没有点过。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +refused_curse_work; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 12; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig买不起规定的木料，用废包装箱的薄板代替。模型在展示当天就塌了，Berta Winter没有骂，只让Hedwig把失败原因写清楚。Hedwig画了七页受力分析，最后发现是节点胶水不够，连夜重做了一个节点。重做的节点在第二次展示时没有裂。
  _effects: cash -2 (工程系的桥梁模型（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +model_collapse_analysis; skill_

- **1932.04** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; flag +rent_installment_plan_

- **1932.06** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Hedwig回说数目对得上才叫信任。Josefine Hofer把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1932.11** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  屋里只有一张床，Hedwig把椅子拼在床边守了三夜，给Josefine Hofer敷药换布。请假扣的工钱让人心疼，但腿伤见好；第四天早上，Josefine Hofer自己端起了粥碗。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004584_

- **1933.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, medical, university
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.01** `state_expire:night-school` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick, state:night-school
  Hedwig的「夜校生」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove night-school state:world-vienna-1912:106_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1933.01** `r13-edu.law-study::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/case-reasoned-both-ways` tags: education, law, university, study, court, medical, knowledge, school
  Hedwig把一桩房租纠纷的判例背得滚瓜烂熟，Luise Stein 2在课上让Hedwig复述判决理由。Hedwig讲到一半，Luise Stein 2打断，问如果换了被告的处境会怎么判。Hedwig想了很久，说可能不一样。Luise Stein 2说，这才是学法律的开头，然后让全班把这个问题抄下来。问题写了满满一页。
  _effects: flag +law_study_progress; skill; education higher_

- **1933.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josefine Hofer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Emil Kral把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Emil Kral说不是Hedwig的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Hedwig心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1933.08** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Franz Kral。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil Kral把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.05 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:132_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  Hedwig和Emil Kral在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Emil Kral把煤钱推过来，Hedwig接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:147; reputation -0.01999999999999999; condition; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.recommendation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/recommendation-secured` tags: education, recommendation, teacher, application, favor, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Weiss把信纸铺开，问Hedwig想申请哪里。Hedwig说完，Josef Weiss写了三页，最后一句是“此人有独立判断”。Hedwig站在旁边，看见Josef Weiss把笔尖蘸了两次墨水，心里比考试还紧张，出门才发现自己一直屏着气。那封信Hedwig抄了一份留着，原件寄出前看了三遍。
  _effects: flag +recommendation_secured; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1934.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Hedwig没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Anna Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中2.7克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:159_

  Emil Kral把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold_

  _effects: skill_

  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

  Gustav Hofer请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Gustav Hofer把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-82_

  Hedwig在雪地里捡到一只缝线开裂的钱包，里面有三克朗、一张彩票和一张写着地址的纸。Hedwig按地址送回去，开门的老太太说钱包不是她的，却把彩票塞给Hedwig，说“你拿着吧，我这辈子没中过”。

  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/ledger-on-table` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Hedwig把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Anton的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Hedwig在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +medicine_bill_settled_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Friedrich Kral的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Friedrich Kral今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 23; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Hedwig在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Hedwig一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 11 -> 17; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig把Ferdinand Kral的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Ferdinand Kral今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

- **1939.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.03999999999999998; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/flour-and-oil` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Grete Winter 3听完没有去开抽屉，只说钱都压在铺子的货上，转手从厨房拎出一袋面粉和半瓶油，让Hedwig带回去。Hedwig在门口站了一会儿，道谢时嗓子发干，下楼时那袋面粉压着胳膊，回去的路比来时沉。
  _effects: food; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Hedwig，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Hedwig说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Hedwig自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 17 -> 29; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007829_

- **1939.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Hedwig摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Grete Winter 3第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Hedwig只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1939.12** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007691_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Grete Kral；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Emil Kral把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:227_

- **1940.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/it-turns-in-the-hand` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Hedwig收了钱，却在灵堂里试着把那个名字指向Grete Winter 3。蜡烛刚点着，Hedwig自己的手先抖起来，火苗顺着袖口爬上去。Grete Winter 3扑灭了火，没有喊人，只把门打开。Hedwig走出去时，听见身后有人在数剩下的蜡烛。
  _effects: reputation -0.07000000000000006; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.6 -> 0.7; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:228; flag +curse_recoiled_on_holder_

  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Emil Kral排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Hedwig，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Hedwig看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001314:child_

  Hedwig在歌剧院门口的雪水里捡到一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面有九克朗、一张当晚的站票和一张调琴师的名片。Hedwig把钱包交到售票处，Emil Kral赶来时没有数钱，只问Hedwig会不会弹琴。三天后，Emil Kral替Hedwig调好了一架旧钢琴，没收工钱，还写了一张去乐器行的介绍信。
  _effects: cash 2 (失主给的谢礼); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; reputation 0.030000000000000027; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 35 -> 25; skill; flag +returned_a_lost_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  开口时只提了手推车，钱的事咽回去。对方爽快地把车钥匙样的铁钩递过来，说用完了搁回院里就行。十克朗改去当铺想办法，一件旧外套和一只怀表抵了十二克朗，赎期三个月，票据折好收在证件夹里。
  _effects: cash 12 (当铺抵当旧外套和怀表换来的钱); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:240; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1941.08** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Hedwig托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Hedwig晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Hedwig却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.08000000000000002 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Berta Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Emil Kral把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Emil Kral在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:252; reputation -0.06000000000000005; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Emil Kral在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Emil Kral。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 4 -> 5; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中9克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Grete Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Emil Kral攥着Hedwig的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1942.09** `r15-love.borrowed-room::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/refuses` tags: love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil Kral在第三次见面时说不能再这样。Hedwig没有挽留，只把对方落下的手套收进抽屉；后来每次打开抽屉，都能看见那双手套，却想不起最后一次见面时对方穿的是什么外套。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +refused_secret_meeting_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Gustav Kral。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil Kral把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1943.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Friedrich Winter请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Friedrich Winter把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.040000000000000036; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

  Hedwig退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Hedwig又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Maria Weiss说不用再吃药，药是给不信的人准备的。Hedwig把三包药粉倒进灶灰，第三天开始说胡话。Emil Kral跑去请Mitzi Berg，Mitzi Berg进门时看见床头还挂着Maria Weiss给的布条，上面用红墨水画着眼睛。Hedwig的嘴唇已经干裂。

  Anton病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Anton走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。

  Luise Stein 2在周日午餐后把一叠德文校样推到Hedwig面前，说“先替我看一遍，署不署名以后再说”。Hedwig翻到第三页，发现自己的脚注已经被并进正文。月底领到第一笔助学金时，Friedrich Moser 2在厨房门口洗碗，水声停了很久。
  _effects: cash 4 (教授家的周日午餐); flag +accepted_assistantship; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; knowledge “the professor merges student footnotes into his own text”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

  _effects: cash -2 (最后一分钟的球); condition; flag +injured_in_match; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000283:child; knowledge “a loose stone sits at the corner of the pitch”_

  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Elisabeth Kral；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Emil Kral把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:295_

- **1945.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1945.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.abandon-project/project-sold` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  房租催到门口，Hedwig把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Friedrich Winter问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Hedwig没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 5; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 53; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010389_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Elisabeth Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010618_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Katharina Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:306_

- **1946.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Rosa Lechner把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Rosa Lechner在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:307; reputation -0.06000000000000005; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Rosa Lechner第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 5 -> 6_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010988_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Elisabeth Kral这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `r13-fam.teach-parent-to-read::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::teach-parent-reform/night-class` tags: task, care, literacy, parent, school, intergenerational, vienna, urban
  市政厅办起了工人夜校，Hedwig替Josefine Hofer去报名。管登记的人问年龄，Hedwig报小了五岁；Josefine Hofer在旁边扯她的袖子，最后自己说“四十七”。第一晚回家，老人把发的铅笔用布包好，说老师夸Josefine Hofer握笔稳。
  _effects: cash -1 (在厨房桌上写自己的名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +enrolled_parent_in_night_school; role; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the adult night school takes workers after the evening shift”; skill; condition_

- **1946.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Ferdinand Bauer说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Ferdinand Bauer陪着Hedwig去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Kral。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil Kral把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Hedwig躺在床上出了一身虚汗。

  _effects: condition; flag +wedding_postponed; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002418:child; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Maria Stein嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Maria Stein家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Maria Stein那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000549:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  战后，债券兑付的通知贴在银行门口，按一百比一换新币。Hedwig把一叠债券摊在柜台上，Gustav Stein数完说“够买一双鞋”。Ferdinand Bauer在后面笑了一声，说“还不如当时买面粉”；Hedwig把换来的钱折好，真的去买了鞋。
  _effects: cash 2 (爱国债券的第六期); flag +war_bonds_redeemed_at_fraction; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; knowledge “war bonds were redeemed at a fraction of face value”_

  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001012:child_

- **1949.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Emil Kral在楼梯间坐了很久；Hedwig在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Hedwig的名字；此后邻居提起Hedwig，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1950.10** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Berta Weiss 4一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-122_

- **1950.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013252_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Grete Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:390_

- **1951.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `r1-civ.refugee-hostel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/considered-emigration-overseas` tags: migration, exile, refugee, work, language, vienna, urban, postwar
  Friedrich Moser说Hedwig的临时证件只能住到月底，之后要么找到工作，要么去另一个城市。Hedwig在街上走了一整天，鞋底磨薄了；傍晚回到旅馆，Anna Huber说有一艘船招厨房帮工，去南美，签三年。Hedwig把船票钱数了两遍，还差一半。
  _effects: cash -1 (难民旅馆的登记簿（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044; condition; flag +considered_emigration_overseas; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002763:child; knowledge “the temporary permit expires at the end of the month”_

  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Kral这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  Hedwig把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Elisabeth Hofer 2赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Hedwig给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅（其中1克朗记入债务）); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1952.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Hermann Huber在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.05** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig端着一只旧锅去敲Franz Huber的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Franz Huber让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Hedwig连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004215:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014295_

- **1953.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Grete Huber在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:426; reputation -0.06000000000000005; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004595:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Grete Huber在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Grete Huber。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004595:child_

  _effects: health -0.18 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 6 -> 7; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi Lechner把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Hedwig去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Maria 2给Hedwig一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Hedwig把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002699:child_

- **1953.05** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/keep-watch-borrow-elsewhere` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Elisabeth Kral 3从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1953.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Hedwig转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Rosa Bauer还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001962:child_

  Alois Weiss病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Alois Weiss走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 30 -> 22; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `r13-fam.take-child-to-school::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/child-enrolled` tags: task, family, school, child, enrolment, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig蹲在门口把Katharina Kral的书包带系紧，又把一块面包塞进侧袋。学校里挤了七十多个孩子，Anna Winter按名字点名，点到Katharina Kral时抬头看了一眼。Hedwig在走廊外等到铃响，听见里面开始念字母，才把攥皱的报名纸收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包带系紧一点); flag +child_enrolled; role; education primary; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000258:child_

- **1956.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/it-turns-in-the-hand` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Hedwig收了钱，却在灵堂里试着把那个名字指向Alois Lechner。蜡烛刚点着，Hedwig自己的手先抖起来，火苗顺着袖口爬上去。Alois Lechner扑灭了火，没有喊人，只把门打开。Hedwig走出去时，听见身后有人在数剩下的蜡烛。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (诅咒在灵堂里反噬持有者); reputation -0.0499999999999996; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.7 -> 0.7999999999999999; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:471_

- **1956.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Hedwig翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000559:child; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1956.01** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  那把菠菜根上还带着湿泥，叶子被压得有些蔫。Elisabeth Huber先松了手，说你家人口少，拿半把够了。Hedwig把菜装进篮子时，听见摊主开始收秤砣，铁盘碰得叮当响。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004967:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1956.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:426_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:fm17-cult-initiate/the-circle-year/signs-again` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  会长让Hedwig在一张空白纸上签名，说这是“把旧名字交出去”。签完，Hedwig才发现那页纸被夹进了会员名册；这个月的奉献又涨了一克朗，没有人解释为什么。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 45 -> 53; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 25 -> 31; cash -1 (上涨的月度奉献)_

- **1956.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Maria Hofer 2忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Hedwig陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Maria Hofer 2站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1956.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Anna Bauer问是谁动的。Anna Bauer说是Franz Winter 2嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1957.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Hedwig的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Hedwig看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016566_

- **1957.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Kral这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 19 -> 14; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 60; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `state_expire:fm17-cult-initiate` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  Hedwig的「晨星会的新成员」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:456; flag +fm17_cult_left_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/whispers-fade` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  一年过去，Hedwig没有再点蜡烛，也没有再去那家后间。教堂的登记册翻到了新的一页，旧页边缘留着指甲划过的痕迹；街上的人开始讨论别的事。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 30 -> 22; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/childcare-arranged` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  Hedwig天没亮就把Katharina Kral裹好，抱到楼下Anton Stein家。Anton Stein接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Hedwig看灶上温着的米汤。Hedwig把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Anton Stein数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Hedwig听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000687:child; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

- **1957.03** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Hermann Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Hermann Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1957.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Hedwig只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1957.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family, injury
  Hedwig和Elisabeth Kral 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1957.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Kral这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017333_

- **1958.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/investigation` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Friedrich Moser来问过一次话。问题围绕Berta Kral的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Friedrich Moser把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:505_

- **1958.01** `state_expire:suspected-witchcraft` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:471_

- **1958.01** `r13-work.apply-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/loan-granted` tags: commerce, credit, bank, guarantor, capital, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Stein把申请单看了两遍，问Hedwig拿什么抵押。Emil Bauer把自己的房契复印件放在柜台上，说这条街上的铺子不能倒。Gustav Stein让两人分别按了指印，批了八十克朗，分十二个月还。出门时Hedwig把钞票缝进内袋，针脚走得很密。
  _effects: cash 25 (银行柜台前的担保人); flag +work13_obtained_a_bank_loan; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004196:child; knowledge “the loan is repayable over twelve months at five percent”_

- **1958.03** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  前面还有七八个人时，Grete Huber已经直起腰把锅盖扣上了。队伍没散，有人骂了一句，有人蹲下把罐子收进布袋。Hedwig走到巷口，Franz Winter 2从后面赶上来，罐子还温着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-139_

- **1959.01** `fm17-cult.recruitment::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/signs-for-the-childs-medicine` tags: faith, cult, recruitment, family, child, school, money, curse
  Katharina Kral夜里咳得厉害，医生说肺弱，要花钱买一种苦药。Gustav Moser 3第二天就送来一小包褐色粉末，说是会里替人祈来的。Hedwig不信那盏灯，却还是在入会纸上按了手印；药粉冲开以后，Katharina Kral确实睡了一夜整觉。
  _effects: state_add fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:518; cash -3 (入会费与药钱); health 0.020000000000000018 (褐色粉末让孩子睡了一夜); flag +fm17_cult_medicine_bargain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1959.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Katharina Kral的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Kral今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 14 -> 22; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 60 -> 57; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1959.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Hedwig的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:505_

- **1959.01** `r13-work.price-goods::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/undercut-the-row` tags: commerce, pricing, market, competition, shop, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig把糖和面粉的价签各擦低一赫勒，粉笔字写得比谁都大。Alois Kral拎着篮子转了一圈，回来把两包糖都放进Hedwig的柜台。对街的Berta Stein看见，把自己的价签也改了；到傍晚，整条街的糖价都落回原处，只有Hedwig的秤盘空得最快。
  _effects: cash 8 (粉笔写的价签); flag +work13_undercut_the_row; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001113:child_

- **1959.02** `r3-war.occupied-bells::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/surrendered-copper` tags: war, occupation, requisition, bells, resistance, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig带人把铜锅、门把手和教堂的烛台堆在广场上，Franz Winter逐个过秤。Gustav Stein想把曾祖父的怀表留下，被Hedwig按住手；Anna Winter把自己的铜十字架也放进堆里，说“称这个的时候轻一点”。
  _effects: cash -4 (钟楼上的征用令); flag +surrendered_copper_to_occupier; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000919:child; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000258:child_

- **1959.07** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Hedwig点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Maria Hofer 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1959.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Otto Huber追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005388:child; food_

- **1960.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Hedwig没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018421_

  Hedwig带着Katharina Kral去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Katharina Kral一直捏着那张课程表，Hedwig则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 57 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 22 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 30 -> 38; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 35 -> 41; cash -1 (上涨的月度奉献); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Gustav Stein把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Hedwig去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Elisabeth Huber给Hedwig一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Hedwig把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999994 (住院处窗口的押金); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004967:child_

- **1960.02** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.fish-heads` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  鱼头用报纸包着，报纸上还印着昨天的广告。Josef Berg一边刮冰一边说，回去多搁姜，别舍不得醋。Hedwig把纸包拎在手里，能感觉到凉气从指缝里钻出来，走到家时报纸已经洇湿了一层。
  _effects: food; health 0.020000000000000018 (喝上了热鱼汤)_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +false_accusation; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:540_

  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Elisabeth Kral 2第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (the curse turns back on its holder); state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 7 -> 8_

  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Rosa Kral 2攥着Hedwig的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.07999999999999996 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1961.01** `fm17-cult.exit-cost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stays-for-the-child` tags: faith, cult, exit, family, child, money, belonging, vienna
  Hedwig已经把行李收到门口，Emil Kral却从院里跑回来，说学堂里有人答应教他写字，还说外面的孩子会笑他没有父亲。Hedwig在门槛上坐了很久，最后把行李又提回屋里。第二天，Hedwig照常去后厅摆椅子。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 38 -> 48; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 41 -> 56; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.doubt 55 -> 65; flag +fm17_cult_stayed_for_child_

- **1961.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Kral这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1961.01** `state_tick:fm17-cult-initiate/the-circle-year/signs-again` tags: fm17-cult-initiate, faith, cult, belonging, obedience, doubt, exit, religion
  会长让Hedwig在一张空白纸上签名，说这是“把旧名字交出去”。签完，Hedwig才发现那页纸被夹进了会员名册；这个月的奉献又涨了一克朗，没有人解释为什么。
  _effects: state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.obedience 48 -> 56; state_parameter fm17-cult-initiate.exitCost 56 -> 62; cash -1 (上涨的月度奉献)_

- **1961.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Friedrich Moser在门口替Hedwig捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1961.03** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Hedwig把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019820_

- **1961.10** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Hedwig让Friedrich Moser记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Friedrich Moser只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1961.11** `af17-humiliation.puppet-show::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/left-the-bazaar` tags: humiliation, public, scandal, theatre, charity, reputation, absurd, vienna
  第三幕开始前，Hedwig从侧门走出市集，围巾遮到眼睛。外面正在下冻雨，马车夫要价一克朗，Hedwig没有还价。第二天，Elisabeth Kral 3在报纸上写“被自己的影子追着跑的人”，文章不刻薄，却让Hedwig在公寓门口站了很久才进去。
  _effects: cash -1 (冻雨里的马车); condition; flag +fled_the_puppet_show_

- **1962.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Helene Bauer病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Helene Bauer走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004832:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019162_

- **1962.01** `fm17-cult.exit-cost::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/the-price-of-the-door` tags: faith, cult, exit, family, child, money, belonging, vienna
  Luise Moser 3没有拦Hedwig，只把一张清单推到桌子中间：学堂的煤、祷告会的灯油、替Hedwig家“挡灾”的七次仪式费用。Hedwig把首饰和这个月的工钱都留下，牵着Katharina Kral走到门口。Katharina Kral回头问，那本赞美诗能不能带走；Luise Moser 3说，书是会的。
  _effects: state_remove fm17-cult-initiate state:world-vienna-1912:518; cash -4 (退出时被要求付清的煤、灯油与仪式费用); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-185; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-185_

- **1962.01** `r13-fam.return-hometown::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::hometown-postwar/family-gone` tags: task, family, return, hometown, migration, relative, vienna, urban
  Hedwig回到老街区，老屋的屋顶塌了一角，院子里长满荨麻。Anton从城外赶来，说家里的人逃的逃、散的散，只剩自己还认得这块地。两人在门槛上坐了一会儿，Hedwig把箱子打开，里面是给每个人带的礼物，已经不知道该给谁。
  _effects: cash -2 (火车进站时看见旧钟楼); mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +returned_to_an_empty_home; relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “most of the family scattered during the war”_

- **1962.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (锥子扎伤拇指根); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1962.10** `r2-law.plagiarism-suit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university::r2-law.plagiarism-suit-reform/expert-supports-subject` tags: law, plagiarism, court, reputation, authorship, vienna, urban, postwar
  新程序允许双方各请一位专家。Hedwig请的语文学教授把两段文字拆成词频表，说相似处都是常用搭配。Anna Winter的专家拍桌子，说词频不能代替阅读。Gustav Stein让两人各写一份意见附卷，判决延到秋天。
  _effects: cash -10 (抄袭案的第二版); reputation 0.06000000000000005; flag +expert_witness_supported_originality; knowledge “the expert report turns on common phrase frequency”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

- **1962.11** `r3-mig.property-expropriation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::reform-property-review/piano-listed-worthless` tags: migration, property, expropriation, home, politics, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig凑了二十克朗，请Friedrich Moser把钢琴写成“损坏，无残值”。Friedrich Moser在清单上改了两个字，收了钱，说“三个月内别弹”。Hedwig等到夜里，用一块毛毯蒙住琴，弹了半首曲子，每个音都像偷来的。
  _effects: cash -20 (贴了封条的钢琴); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +piano_listed_as_worthless; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000289:child; knowledge “a bribe can change an inventory entry to “no residual value””_
